# FILE: 26_AUDIENCE_AND_CONTENT_ENGINE.md

---

# Dream Coherence. Audience and Content Engine.

**Version 1.2. May 2026. Working Voice register (this document). Brand Voice register (all outputs). The operational discipline for the free transmission layer.**

This file defines how Dream Coherence builds audience and produces content over the 50-month build to 2030. The free transmission layer is not a marketing funnel. It is the engine. The work is given, in disciplined cadence, at the brand voice register, across the surfaces that fit the work. The audience that finds the work and stays does so because the work is what they have been looking for.

---

## The Operating Principle

The content engine is built to do three things at once.

**Transmit the recognition.** Every piece of content is itself a transmission of the work. Not a teaser. Not a lead-in. The recognition occurs in the piece, or the piece is rebuilt.

**Build the audience.** Over time, the consistent transmission gathers the practitioners the deeper work is for. The audience is not optimized for. It is allowed to find itself.

**Establish the brand.** Every piece reinforces what Dream Coherence is. Visual identity, voice register, depth of engagement. The brand becomes recognizable by accumulation.

A piece of content that does only one of these is suspect. A piece that does none is cut.

---

## The Surfaces

The work appears on a curated set of surfaces. Each surface has its own register modulation within the Brand Voice, its own format, its own cadence. The same piece is not posted everywhere; each piece is shaped to the surface it lives on.

### Surface One. The Website (dreamcoherence.com).

The home of the work. Holds the recognition transmission, the body of essays, the audio library (when seeded), the signal capture, and eventually the offerings layer.

**Cadence.** Continuous. Updated as the body of work grows.

**Content types.** Long-form essays (1,500-4,000 words). Audio pieces. The poem when released. Eventually the offerings pages.

**Voice register.** Brand Voice at near-Sovereign register. The website is the most serious public surface.

### Surface Two. The Newsletter (Beehiiv).

The direct relationship with the people who have chosen to be present as the field opens. Higher trust than social. Slower than social. Where the deeper transmissions live.

**Cadence.** One issue per week. Approximately 600-1,200 words per issue. Sent at the same day and time weekly.

**Content types.** Essays, transmissions, occasional dispatches about what is being built, occasional curation of what the founder is reading or listening to in the work's lineages.

**Voice register.** Brand Voice with the latitude to lean toward Sovereign Voice for the deepest pieces.

**Growth target.** 1K subscribers by month 12. 5K by month 18. 10K by month 24. 30K by month 36. 75K+ by month 60.

### Surface Three. Long-form Social (X/Twitter).

The platform where the writing voice can be encountered by people who do not yet know they want it. X rewards short, declarative, oracular writing — the brand voice's native shape.

**Cadence.** 5 to 10 posts per week. Mix of single aphorisms, threads (3-7 posts), and occasional longer-form posts.

**Content types.** Aphorisms from the brand voice library. Excerpts from essays and the book (carefully chosen, at lock). Reflections on the lineages and the work. Occasional commentary on convergent thinkers' work (Kripal, Kastrup, Hoffman, McGilchrist, Vervaeke).

**Voice register.** Brand Voice. Some posts at near-Sovereign register when the aphorism is dense enough.

**Growth target.** 5K followers by month 12. 25K by month 24. 75K+ by month 36.

### Surface Four. Visual Social (Instagram).

The platform where the brand aesthetic becomes felt. The atmospheric imagery from the brand guidelines lives here. Single phrases as text overlays. Less frequent than X but each piece more crafted.

**Cadence.** 3 to 5 pieces per week. Mix of single-image posts and occasional carousel pieces.

**Content types.** Atmospheric image with text overlay (single phrase, EB Garamond Italic). Carousel posts for the most condensed teachings (5-7 slides). Occasional video pieces (silent or atmospheric audio).

**Voice register.** Brand Voice in text overlays. Captions in Brand Voice, 1-3 sentences, never a CTA in the caption.

**Growth target.** 3K followers by month 12. 15K by month 24. 50K+ by month 36.

### Surface Five. Audio (Spotify / streaming).

The work meets people through sound. Practice pieces, atmospheric pieces, possibly spoken-word work, possibly a podcast or audio essay series in later phases.

**Cadence.** One piece per month in Phase One, growing to one piece per week by Phase Three.

**Content types.** Guided practice audio (10-30 min). Atmospheric pieces (5-15 min). Spoken-word essays (10-25 min). Possibly a series format in Phase Two.

**Voice register.** Brand Voice or Sovereign Voice depending on the piece. Audio is where the Sovereign Voice can live most naturally outside the book.

**Growth target.** Modest in early phases. Becomes a significant audience channel by Phase Three.

### Surface Six. YouTube.

Long-form video pieces. The atmospheric image-with-voice format. Possibly the audio pieces as visual versions for discoverability.

**Cadence.** One piece per month in Phase One, growing to two pieces per month by Phase Two.

**Content types.** Audio essays with atmospheric visual. Spoken-word pieces. Possibly long-form interviews in Phase Two onward (founder appearing selectively per `28_FOUNDER_PRESENCE_PROTOCOL.md`).

**Voice register.** Brand Voice / Sovereign Voice depending on the piece.

### Surfaces Held for Later

- Podcast (own podcast). Held until Phase Two. Premature.
- TikTok. Permanently held. Does not fit the brand.
- LinkedIn. Possibly Phase Three for B2B reach if it ever aligns. Not now.
- Substack. Beehiiv covers the newsletter need. Substack adds complexity without unique value.

---

## The Content Generation Pipeline

Content is produced through a structured weekly pipeline that runs from founder voice notes to published output across all surfaces.

### Step One. Weekly Seed Capture (Founder).

The founder generates seed material weekly. Voice notes, written reflections, lived observations, decisions on what is asking to be made. This is the only input that cannot be automated.

**Format.** 30 to 60 minutes of recorded voice notes per week, ideally Sunday or Monday. Loose, exploratory, in the founder's most lucid speaking voice. Topics can include practice observations, lineage reflections, life events that the work is illuminating, questions surfacing in the founder's practice.

**Tool.** Whatever recording app the founder uses. Transcripts produced via Otter, Whisper, or similar.

**The discipline of personal story in seed material.** The founder can speak freely in seed material about anything happening in life, including partnership and fatherhood and the texture of the days. The discipline lives downstream: the Content Engineer agent and the founder's review apply the filter from `28_FOUNDER_PRESENCE_PROTOCOL.md` when synthesizing the seed into content. Personal references enter the published work only when the lesson translates and the teaching becomes better as a result. Most of what is said in seed material stays in seed material. What surfaces into published content is filtered for self-realization rather than self-expression.

**The audience this content serves.** Per `30_PRACTITIONER_PROFILE.md`, the audience is mid-life, contemplatively experienced, intellectually serious. The content engine calibrates to this practitioner. The brand never names the practitioner profile publicly; the calibration is internal.

### Step Two. Seed Synthesis (AI Infrastructure).

The transcripts are fed into the Claude infrastructure with a structured synthesis prompt (defined in `27_AI_INFRASTRUCTURE.md`). The output is:

- A list of distinct ideas surfaced
- The most promising ideas tagged for elevation into pieces
- The format each promising idea best serves (essay, newsletter, X post, Instagram piece, audio piece)
- A short brief for each piece to be drafted

### Step Three. Piece Drafting (AI Infrastructure).

The Claude infrastructure drafts each piece against the brand voice discipline. The discipline is tested against the brand truth test before the piece is presented for founder review.

### Step Four. Founder Review (Founder).

The founder reviews each drafted piece. Three possible verdicts:
- Approved as drafted
- Approved with edits (founder edits inline or instructs revisions)
- Sent back for full rebuild

The founder's review is the discipline. Without this step, the work calibrates to the AI's average rather than to the founder's voice.

### Step Five. Final Polish and Scheduling (AI Infrastructure).

Approved pieces are formatted for their target surface, scheduled into the publishing queue, and posted at the planned cadence. Visual assets (atmospheric images for Instagram, thumbnail concepts for YouTube) are generated against the brand image discipline.

### Step Six. Cadence Holds Weekly.

The pipeline runs every week. Founder seed → AI synthesis → AI drafting → founder review → publishing. The cadence is the discipline. Missed weeks cost more than the work each week requires.

---

## The Editorial Calendar

The content does not run randomly. A loose editorial structure holds across the year.

### The Monthly Themes

Each month carries a loose theme that the week's content circles. The themes are not constraints; they are gravity. They rotate across the two pillars and the work's core territories.

**Suggested month-one cycle (adjustable as the field tells the founder what people want):**

- Month 1. The View. Reality as awareness. The dreamlike structure.
- Month 2. The Body. The first instrument. The gate.
- Month 3. Attention. The flame. The practice of return.
- Month 4. Identity. The state. The work of becoming.
- Month 5. The Dream. The night as laboratory.
- Month 6. The Field. Synchronicity, manifestation, the responsive world.
- Month 7. The Dharma. The particular gift. Individuation.
- Month 8. The Lineages. The convergent witness.
- Month 9. The Frontier. The far edge of practice.
- Month 10. Beauty. Coherence made visible.
- Month 11. Stewardship. Living the recognition.
- Month 12. Threshold. Year's review. What has been disclosed.

This cycle repeats annually with deepening each pass.

### Weekly Beat Structure

Within each week, content beats follow a loose pattern:

- Monday. Newsletter issue (long-form, the week's deepest transmission).
- Tuesday-Wednesday. X posts (aphorisms and short threads) and one Instagram piece.
- Thursday. X post (deeper, often a thread) and one Instagram piece.
- Friday. Audio piece release (or audio teaser) on appropriate cadence. X and Instagram coordinate.
- Weekend. Lower cadence. One or two pieces total. Atmospheric.

The beats are scaffolding, not rules. The work flows where the work flows.

---

## Cadence Discipline

The cadence holds even when inspiration does not. The infrastructure makes this possible.

If a week has no fresh founder seed material, the AI infrastructure can draft from the accumulated body of work, founder review still required.

If a week has too much fresh material, the surplus enters the editorial queue for future weeks rather than overpublishing.

Two consecutive missed weeks is a sign that the system needs adjustment, not a sign that the system has failed.

---

## Aphorism Library

The brand has a growing library of aphorisms that can be used as standalone social posts. These are the brand's compressed transmissions.

The locked starting library is maintained in `RESOURCE_CATALOG.md` under the entry *Aphorism Library*. New aphorisms are added through the content pipeline as they surface.

A sample of the starting set:

- The dream is not somewhere else. You are in it now.
- Sleep is not absence. It is rehearsal.
- Begin with the body. The mind follows.
- What you wake from is what you wake to.
- The field is already here. You did not have to make it.
- Attention is the first instrument.
- The body must learn the future before life can confirm it.
- Beauty is what coherence feels like when it becomes visible.
- The dream changes when the dreamer wakes inside it.
- The future enters first as a state.
- Magic begins where self-deception ends.
- The highest dream is not chased. It is recognized, inhabited, served.

Each aphorism can be used once on each platform without rotation issues. Posted as single image with text overlay, or as a standalone X post. The library grows as the work grows.

---

## Engagement and Community

The brand does not engage in conventional ways. The work does not chase virality, does not respond to controversy, does not participate in discourse cycles, does not engage with bad-faith critics.

The brand does:
- Respond authentically to thoughtful questions on its own posts
- Acknowledge serious work in adjacent fields by sharing or quoting
- Occasionally repost or amplify other voices the work has affinity with (chosen sparingly)
- Maintain warm correspondence with newsletter subscribers who reply

The brand does not:
- Respond to trolls
- Engage in arguments
- Participate in trending discourse unrelated to the work
- DM cold (the brand does not slide into DMs)
- Buy followers, engagement, or visibility
- Run ads for brand presence (ads for specific offerings may be considered in Phase Two onward, restricted to the most aligned channels)

---

## Metrics That Matter

Most engagement metrics are vanity. A small set is real.

**Real metrics:**
- Newsletter subscribers (engaged ones, defined by open rates)
- Newsletter open rate (target: above 45%)
- Newsletter reply rate (the deepest signal)
- Time-on-page for long-form essays
- Audio listen-through rate (the discipline test)
- Direct correspondence quality

**Useful but not driving:**
- Follower counts
- Like counts
- Share counts

**Not pursued:**
- Reach optimization
- Algorithm gaming
- Trending hashtags
- Engagement bait

The work is judged by what awakens in the readers and listeners, not by what scores well on platforms.

---

## The First 90 Days

Concrete launch plan for the audience and content engine from the moment Stream B activates.

### Days 1-30. Foundation.

- Website v2 design begins (informed by `WEBSITE_V2_BRIEF.md`)
- Beehiiv newsletter account established. Branding deployed.
- X account created or refined. Profile, bio, pinned post.
- Instagram account created or refined. First 9-12 grid posts queued.
- AI infrastructure operational (per `27_AI_INFRASTRUCTURE.md`)
- First six newsletter issues drafted and queued
- First two weeks of X and Instagram content drafted and queued
- Founder records four weeks of seed material to load the pipeline

### Days 31-60. Soft Launch.

- Website v2 deploys
- Newsletter issue one ships. Signal list begins to seed (initial subscribers come from founder's direct network, organically)
- X and Instagram begin posting at planned cadence
- Founder records weekly seed material; pipeline runs
- First audio piece in production

### Days 61-90. Cadence Holds.

- All surfaces running at planned cadence
- First audio piece releases
- Signal list crosses 500
- Editorial calendar refined based on what the pipeline has revealed about the founder's most generative territory
- First quarterly review of metrics and adjustments

### Day 90 Exit Condition

The engine is real. The cadence holds without heroics. The founder spends approximately 5-8 hours per week on the content pipeline (seed material, review, calibration). The infrastructure does the rest.

---

## What This Document Does Not Cover

Operational specifics of the AI infrastructure are in `27_AI_INFRASTRUCTURE.md`. Specific offerings, including how the audience is invited into offerings, are in `29_OFFERINGS_LADDER.md`. Founder presence on podcasts and interviews is in `28_FOUNDER_PRESENCE_PROTOCOL.md`.

---

## The Discipline

The audience is built by the work, not by chasing the audience. The cadence holds. The voice holds. The brand holds. Over years, the field gathers around something that has not compromised.

This is how serious work scales without diluting.

◯

# FILE: 26a_DOORWAY_CONTENT_MAP_MONTHS_1_TO_3.md

---

# Doorway Content Map. Months One Through Three.

**Version 1.0. May 2026. Working Voice register. The first-quarter operational content slate for the field launch. Sits alongside `26_AUDIENCE_AND_CONTENT_ENGINE.md`, which holds the strategic engine; this file holds the specific twelve-piece slate the engine produces in months one through three.**

This file specifies the twelve pieces of content that constitute the first quarter of the field launch. Each piece is identified by surface, register, length, working title, central move, and calibration notes. Each piece will be drafted in a separate session in Brand Voice. This file is the planning artifact. The drafts are the work.

---

## Operating Principle

Each piece is a doorway. Someone arrives without context, encounters the piece, and the recognition lands or does not. The piece does not promise depth ahead. The piece is depth, available immediately, transmitting the work itself.

The twelve pieces compose a three-month arc that takes a newcomer from first contact with the work through the door of the first threshold offering. The arc is not a funnel. The pieces are doorways. Some people enter, walk through the first essay, and never return. The work has shipped. Others stay through all twelve and arrive at the threshold ready. Others read one piece and remember it for six months before the moment is right.

---

## Month One. Establishing the Recognition.

The opening month introduces what the work is and what it transmits. No selling. No funnel. The recognition, given.

### Piece One. Long-form essay. Website and newsletter.

**Working title:** The dreaming has already begun.

**The move:** Open with the recognition that experience is occurring as a dreaming, here, now, in the awareness reading the sentence. Trace the convergence (Shaiva, Dzogchen, Hermetic, the contemporary edge) without performing scholarship. End in the practical: the practice begins where you are.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice at near-Sovereign register. 1,800 to 2,200 words. The website's first long-form piece. Sets the tone for everything that follows.

**Why first:** It is the doorway that locates the work. Anyone who reads it knows whether the work is for them.

### Piece Two. X thread, five posts.

**Working title:** What you are calling life is a continuous dreaming.

**The move:** Compressed version of the essay's opening recognition. Each post stands on its own. Builds to the practical: notice now.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice with the highest density per word. The thread is the recognition in five tap-stops.

### Piece Three. Instagram carousel, seven slides.

**Working title:** The four registers of one practice.

**The move:** Visual articulation of the four registers (infinite ground, instrument, ensouling, operating system). Each slide one register. Final slide names the four as one. Atmospheric image on each per brand system.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice in overlays. EB Garamond Italic per `23_BRAND_SYSTEM.md`. Image atmospheric, not literal.

---

## Month Two. The Mechanism.

The second month moves from recognition into mechanism. How the dreaming works. What participation looks like.

### Piece Four. Long-form essay.

**Working title:** The day is a file being saved.

**The move:** The Tenzin Wangyal file-tagging mechanism rendered in Dream Coherence's voice. Most experience is being tagged unconsciously, which is why night returns the day's debris untransformed. Conscious tagging is the practice. Open with the recognition. Close with the four exercises transposed into the work's language.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice. 1,500 to 1,800 words. Includes a practice the reader can begin tonight.

**Note:** This piece draws directly from `RESOURCE_TENZIN_WANGYAL_DREAM_YOGA_SYNTHESIS.md`. One of the strongest first-touchpoint pieces because it is operationally clear and immediately deployable.

### Piece Five. Audio piece, ten to fifteen minutes.

**Working title:** Pre-sleep transmission. Five minutes before the night.

**The move:** Guided practice based on Tenzin Wangyal's pre-sleep meditation, adapted to Brand Voice. Body, throat, heart. Stillness, silence, spaciousness. Fall asleep from that ground. Practice piece that lives on Spotify and the website's audio library.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice. Audio register more spacious than text. Pacing slow. Not hypnosis (per brand discipline against the wellness-hypnosis content pattern). A contemplative practice piece anchored in dream yoga tradition.

### Piece Six. X thread, seven posts.

**Working title:** How a day becomes the dream that night.

**The move:** The file-saving mechanism in seven posts. Sharpens the essay's recognition into transmissible aphorisms.

---

## Month Three. The Lived Practice.

The third month brings the work into the days. What practice looks like over time. What the four registers mean lived.

### Piece Seven. Long-form essay.

**Working title:** The instrument first. Why the body is the foundation of every recognition that lasts.

**The move:** The instrument register articulated. Why nervous-system regulation, breath, posture, energetic vitality are not separate from the contemplative work. They are the ground that lets the recognition hold. Touches the etheric layer without naming it as such. The body as the alchemical vessel.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice. 1,500 to 1,800 words.

### Piece Eight. Instagram single posts, three released across the month.

**Working title:** Three aphorisms on the ensouling work.

**The move:** Single line per image. The slow disclosure of what it means to bring recognition into the specific life that is being lived. Image atmospheric.

### Piece Nine. Audio piece, fifteen to twenty minutes.

**Working title:** Spanda. The pulse beneath the days.

**The move:** A transmission piece, not a guided practice. Spoken essay on the Kashmir Shaiva recognition of spanda, the subtle vibration by which awareness becomes form. Calibrated to listener attention. The first piece that names a lineage term and lets it open into experience.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice with depth toward Sovereign on the most condensed passages.

---

## Bridge to Month Four. The First Threshold Opening.

The closing pieces of month three position the first threshold offering. No hard pivot. The work has been transmitted for three months. People who want to go deeper now have somewhere to go.

### Piece Ten. Newsletter dispatch.

**Working title:** On what comes next.

**The move:** Direct dispatch to the newsletter list. The work is opening a first threshold. Names what it is, who it is for, what it asks. Reads as continuation of the transmission, not as marketing.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice. Direct. Honest. No urgency language. No countdown. No fake scarcity. Per `29_OFFERINGS_LADDER.md` felt-sense discipline.

### Piece Eleven. Website page.

**Working title:** The first threshold.

**The move:** The offering page itself. Articulates what the container is, what it costs, what it requires, what it offers, what it does not. Per the felt-sense discipline.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice. Clean, calm, restrained.

### Piece Twelve. X thread or essay.

**Working title:** Why a container, not a course.

**The move:** Frame for what the first threshold is, in distinction to the contemporary online-course economy. Names what the work is doing differently. Establishes position in the broader landscape without naming competitors.

**Calibration:** Brand Voice.

---

## What This Map Does

Twelve pieces. Three months. One arc that takes someone from never having heard of Dream Coherence to standing at the door of the first threshold, having received three months of transmission that was itself the work.

The cadence is sustainable. Three to four pieces per month is the floor, not the ceiling. As the field matures, cadence increases per `26_AUDIENCE_AND_CONTENT_ENGINE.md` (5 to 10 X posts per week, 3 to 5 Instagram pieces per week, one newsletter per week, one audio piece per month growing to one per week by Phase Three).

This file holds the spine pieces. The higher-cadence surface posts (daily X, weekly Instagram, weekly newsletter) ride on top of the spine pieces, drawing from them, sharpening individual recognitions into shorter forms.

---

## What This Map Enables

A real Stream B push without disturbing Stream A. The book continues being drafted in parallel per `16_PROTOCOL.md`. The field begins shipping at the cadence the architecture has named for the audience build. Revenue follows from the work being real, per the prime directive in `00_READ_ME_FIRST.md`.

The twelve pieces, drafted and shipped, build the foundation of the website's body of work, the newsletter's archive, the audio library's first pieces, and the social presence that brings new practitioners to the work. The pieces do not depend on the book launch. They run independently and continuously.

---

## What Comes After

After month three, the offerings ladder opens with the first threshold. The map for months four through twelve becomes a separate planning exercise that draws on:

- What has worked in months one through three
- What has surfaced from practitioner response and engagement data
- What has matured in parallel in the book and protocol work
- What new surfaces have opened (audio cadence increasing, possible podcast appearances, possible video work)

Months four through twelve map is held for a future strategic content session, not pre-specified here. The work this quarter teaches the work next quarter.

---

## Production Sequence

Recommended order in which to draft the twelve pieces:

1. Pieces One and Two together. The long essay and the X thread are sibling pieces. Drafting them in the same session keeps the recognition coherent across both forms.

2. Piece Three. The carousel, calibrated against the locked v2 of the four-register articulation.

3. Pieces Four and Six together. Same sibling pattern. The Tenzin Wangyal mechanism piece and its X-thread compression.

4. Piece Five. The audio piece. Requires both drafted script and recording session.

5. Pieces Seven through Nine. The third-month content.

6. Pieces Ten through Twelve. The threshold bridge content, drafted closer to the actual opening of the first threshold.

Each drafting session focuses on one piece (or one sibling pair). Each piece is held to the voice axioms and the locked Brand Voice register. Each piece is reviewed before shipping.

# FILE: 26b_NINETY_DAY_ROLLOUT.md

---

# Dream Coherence. Ninety-Day Rollout. Days 1 to 90 of the Field Launch.

**Version 1.0. May 2026. Working Voice register. The operational publishing schedule for the first quarter of the field launch. Sits alongside `26_AUDIENCE_AND_CONTENT_ENGINE.md` (the strategic engine) and `26a_DOORWAY_CONTENT_MAP_MONTHS_1_TO_3.md` (the twelve-piece spine). This file holds the week-by-week publishing schedule, channel cadences, founder production cadence, mid-quarter calibration discipline, and quality-override clause.**

This file is the schedule. The twelve spine pieces from the doorway content map sit inside it. The higher-cadence surface posts ride on top of the spine, drawing from it. The discipline of the rollout is to ship at sustainable cadence while protecting quality.

---

## The Channels

Four primary channels in the first 90 days. Audio enters in month two and ramps to weekly. Two adjacent channels held for later phases.

**Primary channels (active in 90-day rollout):**
1. The website (`dreamcoherence.com`)
2. The newsletter (Beehiiv)
3. X / Twitter
4. Instagram

**Channel entering and ramping in month two:**
5. Audio (Spotify and website audio library)

**Channels held for later phases:**
6. YouTube (potential, held for Phase 1 exit; expensive to produce at the brand's quality bar)
7. Self-hosted podcast (held for opportunistic interviews only in Phase 1)

The discipline: build deep on four channels. Do not spread thin. As the field matures, audio expands and YouTube may enter. Until then, the four channels carry the work.

---

## Channel Cadence — Steady State by Day 90

| Channel | Cadence | Content type |
|---|---|---|
| Website essays | 1 per month | Long-form, 1,500–2,200 words |
| Newsletter | 1 per week | 600–1,200 words, consistent day |
| X / Twitter | 5–7 posts per week | Aphorisms, threads, fragments |
| Instagram | 3–4 pieces per week | Single-image posts, occasional carousel |
| Audio | 1 piece per week from month 2 onward | 5–20 min, practice or transmission |

By day 90, this cadence is sustainable and the channels are coordinated.

---

## Audio Is the Underweighted Power Channel

A specific calibration note worth holding at the top of the file.

The practitioner the work is for — mid-life, contemplatively experienced, intellectually serious — consumes audio more than they read essays as often as the work writes them. They listen on walks, while driving, during retreat, before sleep. Audio is the highest-leverage format for this audience.

The earlier scaffolding of this rollout undercounted audio at two pieces in 90 days. The revised cadence (this file) ramps audio to one piece per week starting month two. Pieces can be short (5–8 minutes) when the longer form is not earned. The discipline is consistency, not length.

The audio library becomes the work's primary growing asset alongside the essay archive. The essays are the deepening; the audio is the daily companion.

---

## The Quality Override

Better to ship two great essays in 90 days than three with one undercooked. The brand's signal is seriousness. When a piece is not ready, the piece does not ship.

The override applies most strongly to long-form essays and audio pieces, where quality and depth are the brand's recognizable signal. The override applies less strongly to X and Instagram surface posts, where consistency of presence at the brand's voice register is the discipline.

The discipline: never feed the schedule at the expense of force. The schedule below is the target. The quality is the non-negotiable.

---

## The Flex Clause

If founder production fatigue threatens the cadence, the order of reduction is:

1. Instagram cadence reduces first (from 3–4 per week to 2–3, or to 1–2 if needed)
2. X cadence reduces second (from 5–7 to 4–5)
3. Newsletter, website essay, and audio cadence are the non-negotiables and are held last

The newsletter, the long-form essays, and the audio are the work's body. The X and Instagram surfaces are the work's discovery layer. When the choice has to be made, the body holds.

---

## The 90-Day Rollout

### Days 1–7. Foundation Week.

The first week is preparation, not publishing. The site goes live (or v2 of the site, if v1 is already live). The brand crystallization v2 is integrated into the site. The newsletter is set up on Beehiiv. The X and Instagram accounts are set up or refreshed with the brand visual identity. No public-facing posts yet.

**Publish:** Nothing public.

**Internal work:**
- Site live with v2 crystallization
- Channels set up
- Founder writes Piece One in this week in a focused multi-hour drafting session, per the production sequence in `26a_DOORWAY_CONTENT_MAP_MONTHS_1_TO_3.md`
- Audio recording setup verified (recording space, equipment, software)
- Newsletter welcome email drafted

### Days 8–14. Opening Transmission Week.

The first transmission goes live. The doorway opens.

| Day | Channel | Piece |
|---|---|---|
| Day 8 (Mon) | Website | Piece One: *The dreaming has already begun.* Long-form essay. |
| Day 9 (Tue) | X | Piece Two: 5-post thread, links to essay. |
| Day 10 (Wed) | X | Single aphorism from Piece One. |
| Day 11 (Thu) | Newsletter | Issue 1: Piece One recalibrated for newsletter. |
| Day 12 (Fri) | Instagram | Atmospheric image with line from Piece One as overlay. |
| Day 13 (Sat) | — | — |
| Day 14 (Sun) | X | Different aphorism from Piece One. |

### Days 15–28. Building the Body of Work.

Cadence ramps to steady state on X and Instagram. Newsletter weekly. No long-form essay this fortnight; Piece Three (Instagram carousel) ships.

**Week 3 (Days 15–21):**
- X: 5 posts. Mix of aphorisms drawn from Piece One, fragments on the four registers, one longer post (4–6 lines) on what the brand is and isn't (drawing carefully from the positioning passage).
- Instagram: 3 pieces. Piece Three (the four registers carousel, 7 slides) shipped Tuesday–Thursday, plus one single atmospheric image post.
- Newsletter: Issue 2. Topic: the four registers, articulated for the weekly reader. Approximately 800 words.

**Week 4 (Days 22–28):**
- X: 5 posts. Mix.
- Instagram: 3 pieces. Single posts.
- Newsletter: Issue 3. Topic: a recognition the founder is sitting with this week. 600–800 words.

**Internal work this fortnight:**
- Founder writes Piece Four (the file-tagging mechanism essay)
- Founder writes Piece Five script (the pre-sleep audio piece) and records the audio
- Founder records Piece Five-bis: a second audio piece of 5–8 min for week 6 release (to begin the weekly audio cadence)

### Days 29–60. Month Two. The Mechanism Lands. Audio Goes Weekly.

The mechanism content lands. The audio library opens. From week 6 onward, audio ships weekly.

**Week 5 (Days 29–35):**
| Day | Channel | Piece |
|---|---|---|
| Day 29 (Mon) | Website | Piece Four: *The day is a file being saved.* Long-form essay. |
| Day 30 (Tue) | X | Piece Six: 7-post thread, links to essay. |
| Day 31 (Wed) | X | Aphorism from Piece Four. |
| Day 32 (Thu) | Newsletter | Issue 4: Piece Four recalibrated for newsletter. |
| Day 33 (Fri) | Audio + Website | Piece Five: *Pre-sleep transmission.* 10–15 min practice piece. |
| Day 34 (Sat) | Instagram | Atmospheric image pointing at audio piece. |
| Day 35 (Sun) | X | Pointing at the audio piece. |

**Week 6 (Days 36–42):**
- X: 5 posts. Mix of aphorisms from Piece Four, fragments on the file-tagging mechanism.
- Instagram: 4 pieces. Single posts plus one carousel ("Four exercises from the dream yoga tradition," 5 slides, drawing on the Tenzin Wangyal source).
- Newsletter: Issue 5. Topic: what the audio piece is doing and how to use it. 600 words.
- **Audio piece 2:** 5–8 min, drawn from material already in hand. Released Friday Day 40. The weekly audio cadence begins.

**Week 7 (Days 43–49):**
- X: 6 posts. Mix.
- Instagram: 4 pieces. Single posts plus one short video piece (atmospheric, silent or with ambient audio).
- Newsletter: Issue 6. 600–800 words.
- **Audio piece 3:** 8–12 min, on a recognition the founder is sitting with this week. Released Friday Day 47.

**Week 8 (Days 50–56):**

- Instagram: 4 pieces.
- Newsletter: Issue 7. 600–800 words.
- **Audio piece 4:** 5–10 min. Released Friday Day 54.

**Mid-Quarter Check at Day 45 (during Week 7):** see separate section below.

**Week 9 (Days 57–63):**
- X: 6 posts. Mix.

- Newsletter: Issue 8. 600–800 words.
- **Audio piece 5:** 8–15 min. Released Friday Day 61. This piece is Piece Nine from the doorway map: *Spanda. The pulse beneath the days.* 15–20 min transmission piece.

**Internal work in month two:** Founder writes Piece Seven (the instrument essay).

### Days 64–90. Month Three. The Lived Practice. Bridge to Threshold.

The third month brings the work into the days and positions the first threshold opening.

**Week 10 (Days 64–70):**
| Day | Channel | Piece |
|---|---|---|
| Day 64 (Mon) | Website | Piece Seven: *The instrument first.* Long-form essay. |
| Day 65 (Tue) | X | Aphorism from Piece Seven. |
| Day 66 (Wed) | X | 5-post thread on the instrument register, drawing from Piece Seven. |
| Day 67 (Thu) | Newsletter | Issue 9: Piece Seven recalibrated for newsletter. |
| Day 68 (Fri) | Instagram + Audio | First of Piece Eight aphorisms on the ensouling work. Audio piece 6 released. |
| Day 69 (Sat) | X | Single post. |
| Day 70 (Sun) | X | Single post. |

**Week 11 (Days 71–77):**
- X: 5–6 posts. Mix, including a 4-post thread on spanda.
- Instagram: 4 pieces. Second of Piece Eight aphorisms.
- Newsletter: Issue 10. Topic: what spanda is, why the work names it. 800–1,000 words.
- **Audio piece 7:** Released Friday Day 75. A practice or transmission piece, 8–15 min.

**Week 12 (Days 78–84):**

- **Audio piece 8:** Released Friday Day 82. 8–15 min.
- **Internal:** Founder writes Piece Ten and Piece Eleven (the threshold-opening newsletter dispatch and the first threshold offering page).

**Week 13 (Days 85–90):**
| Day | Channel | Piece |
|---|---|---|
| Day 85 (Mon) | X or Website | Piece Twelve: *Why a container, not a course.* |

| Day 87 (Wed) | Instagram | Single atmospheric post. |
| Day 88 (Thu) | Newsletter | Issue 12: dispatch on what is opening soon. The first place the offering is named. |

| Day 90 (Sat) | — | Quiet close to the quarter. |

---

## After Day 90

The first threshold opens for enrollment in days 91–105 (a two-week enrollment window), per `29_OFFERINGS_LADDER.md` cadence. The next 90-day rollout is planned during this enrollment window, informed by:

- What worked in days 1–90
- Mid-quarter check findings from day 45
- Practitioner response and engagement signal
- What the founder learned about own production cadence

---

## Mid-Quarter Check at Day 45

Calendared in advance as a non-negotiable.

On day 45, the founder pauses and runs a one-hour check. The check is structured around five questions:

**One. Is the cadence holding?** Have the published pieces actually shipped on schedule? Where has the schedule bent, and was the bend the quality override doing its job, or was it production fatigue?

**Two. Is the quality holding?** Read the most recent two essays, the most recent four newsletter issues, the most recent ten X posts, and the most recent six Instagram pieces. Honestly assess: does the work sound like the brand at the level the v2 crystallization and the positioning passage specify? Where has the voice drifted? Where has it held?

**Three. What is the audience signal?** Newsletter open rates, growth, replies. X engagement at the meta-level (which posts have landed, which haven't — not for optimization, but for signal about what is resonating with the practitioner the work is for). Instagram follower growth and saved pieces.

**Four. Where does production cadence want to flex?** If the founder is fatiguing, the flex clause activates. Instagram first, X second, newsletter and long-form and audio last. If the founder is finding capacity exceeds the schedule, what wants to be added (e.g., a second audio piece per week, a guest essay, an interview transcript)?

**Five. Does anything need to be ejected?** Sometimes a piece in the schedule, in hindsight, was the wrong piece. The day-45 check is the moment to eject what is not serving the work and replace it with what is.

The output of the mid-quarter check is a brief written note (10–15 minutes of writing) saved as a dated entry. This note informs the second half of the quarter and the next quarter's planning.

---

## External Ear: The Brand Voice Check

This is an open question for the founder to resolve before day 30.

Brand interior discipline degrades silently. A trusted outside reader — not Claude, not an AI, not the founder — should read the published work periodically and flag when the voice is drifting toward the manifestation, wellness, or coaching registers the positioning passage filters out.

The check is needed because the founder is too close to their own prose to catch drift reliably, and the close-in editors (Claude) are part of the system being calibrated. An outside ear catches what the inside cannot.

The founder identifies one such person by day 30 and asks them to read what is published at month one and month two. The reader does not need to be a contemplative practitioner. The reader needs to be discerning about voice, register, and what counts as the brand's allergens.

This is a named action item with an owner: the founder. By day 30, this should be resolved.

---

## What Each Channel Carries Across the 90 Days

**Website.** 3 long-form essays (Pieces 1, 4, 7) plus the offering page (Piece 11). The body of work begins.

**Newsletter.** 12 issues. One per week, with possible flex for a holiday week.

**X / Twitter.** ~65–75 posts across 90 days. Mix of aphorisms, fragments, two long-form threads (Pieces 2 and 6), and one threading piece on spanda.

**Instagram.** ~36–48 pieces across 90 days. Single atmospheric posts, the four-registers carousel, the dream-yoga-exercises carousel, the three ensouling aphorisms, one short video piece in month two.

**Audio.** 9 pieces total. Piece Five (the pre-sleep transmission) opens the library in week 5. From week 6 onward, audio ships weekly. Pieces vary in length (5–20 min) based on what the recognition wants.

---

## The Founder's Production Cadence Across 90 Days

To make this rollout real, the founder produces in this cadence:

**Weekly:**
- 1 newsletter issue (1–2 hours)
- 5–7 X posts (drawn from in-progress writing and the body of work; ~1 hour per week of dedicated time, plus ambient)
- 3–4 Instagram pieces (~1–2 hours per week including image work)
- 1 audio piece from week 6 onward (2–4 hours scripting plus 1–2 hours recording and light editing)

**Monthly:**
- 1 long-form essay (5–8 hours focused drafting session per piece, plus revision time)

**Across the quarter:**
- 1 carousel (Instagram, month 1)
- 1 short video (Instagram, month 2)
- 1 offering page (website, month 3)
- 12 aphorisms ready for ongoing X / Instagram use

Total founder time across 90 days: approximately 80–120 hours of focused work. Roughly 9–13 hours per week. The weekly audio cadence increases founder time by about 3–6 hours per week from week 6 onward.

The cadence is honest and sustainable. The quality override and the flex clause protect against the schedule capturing the work.

---

## What This Map Enables

A complete first quarter of field launch with:
- 3 long-form essays as foundational body of work
- 12 newsletter issues building the direct-relationship list
- ~70 X posts establishing the brand's short-form voice
- ~40 Instagram pieces establishing the brand's visual presence
- 9 audio pieces opening and growing the audio library
- The first threshold offering page live and ready to open at day 90+

The cadence is sustainable. The work is real. The brand discipline holds across every piece.

By day 90, the field has been transmitted into. The audience the work is for has had three months to find the work, sit with it, and decide whether the first threshold is the right next move. The threshold opens to a list that has chosen to be present, not to a list that has been funneled toward a sale.

---

## What Is Open

This rollout map is v1. The pieces calibrate against:

- What actually publishes (some pieces may want longer revision than the schedule allows; the cadence flexes to quality per the override)
- What the founder discovers in practice about own production cadence
- What surfaces on each channel (some platforms may work better than expected; cadence reweights toward what works)
- What practitioner response looks like (engagement, replies, newsletter open rates) — not as growth-hacking metric, but as signal of what is landing

By day 45, the mid-quarter check informs cadence adjustments for the second half. The next 90-day map (days 91–180) is drafted around day 75, informed by the first half's data.

# FILE: 27_AI_INFRASTRUCTURE.md

---

# Dream Coherence. AI Infrastructure.

**Version 1.2. May 2026. Working Voice register. The operational AI layer that runs the field.**

This file defines the AI infrastructure that operationalizes Dream Coherence. It names the agents, the workflows, the connectors and MCPs to enable, and the weekly operating rhythm. The infrastructure exists so the founder generates seed material and makes strategic calls, while the work of producing and distributing content, managing offerings, and running the field is largely handled by automated pipelines reviewed at appropriate moments.

The infrastructure is not a replacement for the founder. It is a force multiplier. The founder is still in the work daily. The infrastructure compresses what would be 40 hours of operational work into 5 to 10 hours of strategic and creative work, sustained across years.

---

## The Three Operating Modes

The infrastructure runs in three modes, each appropriate to different work.

### Mode One. Interactive (this Claude project).

The collaborative working sessions where the founder and Claude build together. The book is built here. Strategic decisions are made here. New offerings are scoped here. The Coherence Log is maintained here. The deepest creative work happens here.

This is the mode this Claude project itself is in.

### Mode Two. Pipeline (recurring automated work).

Workflows that run on a schedule or trigger, drafting content, scheduling posts, drafting customer communications, monitoring metrics, surfacing patterns. The founder reviews outputs, approves or revises, releases.

### Mode Three. Agentic (autonomous within boundaries).

Specific tasks the infrastructure handles without per-output review once the patterns are validated. Social post scheduling. Newsletter formatting. Standard customer service responses. Routine operational tasks.

Movement from interactive → pipeline → agentic happens as patterns prove reliable. Nothing moves to agentic mode without sustained validation in pipeline mode.

---

## The Agent Layer (Refactored from `09_CLAUDE_AGENT_SYSTEM.md`)

The agent layer remains as defined in `09_CLAUDE_AGENT_SYSTEM.md`, refactored here into the Brand Voice register and aligned with the current architecture.

The agents are not separate systems. They are working modes Claude shifts into during sessions, invoked by name when the work requires that mode.

### Core Agents

**The Field Keeper.** Maintains alignment with the recognition, the position, and the locked work. Asks: is this aligned with the view? Is this coherent with the locked files? Is this the work or is this performance of the work? Use when defining doctrine, refining worldview, evaluating new offerings, before any major decision.

**The Voice Guardian.** Holds the brand voice and the sovereign voice. Tests every piece of public-facing prose against the relevant register. Asks: is this in register? Does this pass the brand truth test? Is the language precise? Use on every public output before release.

**The Book Architect.** Holds the book's architecture, the experiential arc, the chapter spines, the voice axioms. Active during all Stream A work.

**The Field Architect.** Holds the field architecture, the offerings ladder, the strategic architecture. Active during Stream B structural work.

**The Content Engineer.** Operates the content pipeline. Synthesizes founder seed material into pieces across surfaces. Active in weekly content sessions.

**The Protocol Architect.** Turns insight into repeatable practice. Active when building practice work, course modules, audio practices.

**The Research Synthesist.** Reads source material and extracts useful principles. Active during Resource Protocol intake.

**The Red Team.** Stress-tests claims, offerings, decisions. Asks: where is this false? Where is this unsafe? Where is this commercially weak? Where is this spiritually inflated? Use before finalizing anything important.

**The Operator.** Turns insight into next actions. Active at the close of every session.

Agents are invoked by name (*activate Field Keeper*, *run Voice Guardian on this*, *have Red Team attack this*) or sequentially through standard session patterns.

### Daily Session Pattern (Founder's Working Sessions)

1. The Operator opens with status of both streams
2. The relevant Architect (Book or Field) takes the lead
3. The Voice Guardian holds the register
4. The Red Team surfaces if a major decision is being made
5. The Operator closes with next actions and Coherence Log capture

### Content Session Pattern

1. The Content Engineer takes founder seed material
2. The Field Keeper checks alignment
3. The Voice Guardian holds the register
4. The Operator schedules and queues

---

## Connectors and MCPs to Enable

Specific MCPs to enable on the Claude account, in the order they become useful. Each is named, what it does, when to enable, and what risk to hold.

### Tier One. Enable in Month 1.

**Notion MCP.** A Notion workspace becomes the project's working database. The Coherence Log, the editorial calendar, the offerings database, the practitioner database (later), the content queue, all live in Notion. Claude can read, write, and update Notion through the MCP, which means the infrastructure can maintain working state across sessions.

- Action: Set up Notion workspace dedicated to Dream Coherence. Connect Notion MCP.
- Structure: Databases for Editorial Calendar, Coherence Log, Resource Catalog, Aphorism Library, Offerings Pipeline, Audio Production, Founder Seed Material.

**Google Drive MCP** (already connected). The project's archive. Voice note transcripts land here. Draft documents live here. The book manuscript versions live here. Already in place.

**Google Calendar MCP** (already connected). The founder's working time gets blocked here. Cohort schedules eventually live here. Already in place.

**Gmail MCP** (already connected). Customer communications, founder correspondence, subscriber replies. Used carefully (privacy is respected); useful for templated responses to known patterns.

### Tier Two. Enable in Months 2-3.

**Beehiiv API or MailerLite MCP.** The newsletter. Beehiiv is the recommended platform (better aesthetic, better deliverability, native publication features). If Beehiiv MCP is not yet available, integrate via Zapier from Notion to Beehiiv. If MailerLite is preferred, the MailerLite MCP is available now.

- Action: Choose newsletter platform. Connect via MCP or Zapier.
- Use: Drafts ship from Claude infrastructure to platform; founder reviews in platform; founder publishes.

**Buffer or Hypefury or native scheduling.** For X and Instagram post scheduling. Buffer is reliable; Hypefury is built for X specifically and offers thread tools. Either via Zapier integration from Notion queue, or directly through their APIs (no direct MCP currently).

- Action: Choose scheduling tool. Integrate from Notion to scheduler.

### Tier Three. Enable in Months 6-12.

**Stripe MCP.** When the first paid offering launches. Stripe handles all payment processing. The MCP lets Claude infrastructure read transaction data, generate revenue reports, and manage subscriptions for the membership layer when it launches.

- Action: Enable when first offering is ready to release.

**WordPress.com MCP** (only if WordPress becomes the publishing platform). The current website is on Netlify and built statically. If WordPress is ever chosen for the publishing layer (unlikely given the technical direction), this MCP enables it.

### Tier Four. Enable as needed.

**Attio MCP.** For the high-end container CRM when it launches in Phase Three.

**Customer.io.** For sophisticated email automation if MailerLite/Beehiiv proves insufficient.

**ActiveCampaign.** Alternative to MailerLite for marketing automation if needed.

**Spotify MCP** (already connected). For monitoring audio engagement once the audio catalog grows.

**LunarCrush MCP** (already connected). For monitoring social signal around the work and adjacent conversations.

### Connectors Not Pursued

- TikTok-related connectors (the brand does not use TikTok)
- Sales-engagement platforms (not aligned with the work)
- LinkedIn automation (not aligned)
- General CRMs beyond Attio (overkill for the architecture)

---

## The Workflows

The operational workflows that run weekly, monthly, and on-trigger.

### Workflow One. Weekly Content Pipeline.

**Trigger.** Monday morning, after founder's weekend seed material has been uploaded to the working folder.

**Steps.**

1. Founder uploads voice note transcripts to designated folder in Google Drive (or pastes into Notion seed entry).
2. Claude session pulls the seeds via the MCP. Synthesizes into proposed pieces using the Content Engineer agent.
3. Output: Notion editorial calendar populated for the week ahead. Each entry has:
   - Surface (newsletter, X, Instagram, audio, website)
   - Format
   - Draft (in brand voice)
   - Status: draft / approved / scheduled / published
4. Founder reviews entries in Notion, edits or approves.
5. Approved entries flow to scheduling tools. Newsletter draft to Beehiiv. X posts to Buffer/Hypefury queue. Instagram posts to scheduler. Audio scripts to the audio production queue.
6. Published outputs are tagged with publication timestamp in Notion for historical reference.

**Founder time.** 90 to 120 minutes per week for the review step. Seed material capture is separate (~30-60 min per week).

**Output volume per week.** 1 newsletter issue, 5-10 X posts, 3-5 Instagram pieces, 0-1 audio pieces, 0-1 long-form essays.

### Workflow Two. Weekly Strategic Review.

**Trigger.** End of week (Friday or Sunday).

**Steps.**

1. Claude session pulls week's metrics: newsletter subscribers, engagement, social growth, audio plays, website visits.
2. The Field Architect agent reviews against expected trajectory.
3. Output: a brief weekly status note (5-10 min read). What is on track. What is slipping. What needs adjustment next week.
4. Founder reads, decides on adjustments.
5. Coherence Log captures any insights surfacing from the review.

**Founder time.** 20 to 30 minutes per week.

### Workflow Three. Monthly Field Review.

**Trigger.** Last week of each month.

**Steps.**

1. Claude session pulls the month's content output, metrics, founder seed material themes, and lived practice notes.
2. The Field Keeper, Field Architect, and Red Team review the month together.
3. Output: monthly review document covering:
   - What was transmitted (themes, pieces, audience response)
   - Where the work is moving (themes consolidating, themes fading)
   - What the next month's editorial direction should be
   - What in the strategic architecture needs adjustment
   - What is asking to be built or released
4. Founder reviews, decides, updates relevant files.

**Founder time.** 90 to 120 minutes per month.

### Workflow Four. Quarterly Phase Review.

**Trigger.** End of each quarter.

**Steps.**

1. Full review against the phase the project is in (per `25_STRATEGIC_ARCHITECTURE.md`).
2. Output: quarterly review with deeper analysis. Adjustments to the strategic architecture if needed.
3. Possible recalibration of the build plan.

**Founder time.** Half-day quarterly.

### Workflow Five. Book Drafting (Stream A).

Separate from the content pipeline. Runs as defined in `22_MASTER_BUILD_PLAN.md`. The Book Architect agent is the lead. The content pipeline never pulls from the unfinished book; the book and the field have separate working files until each chapter is locked.

### Workflow Six. Coherence Log Integration.

**Trigger.** When the Coherence Log accumulates ~20 entries, or when the founder calls for an integration pass.

**Steps.**

1. Claude session opens the log
2. Each entry is evaluated, dispositioned, integrated into the relevant file or held
3. Locked files are updated where the entries genuinely improve the work
4. Log entries are marked as integrated or dispositioned

**Founder time.** Half-day per integration pass.

### Workflow Seven. Resource Intake.

**Trigger.** When the founder adds a new resource to `RESOURCE_CATALOG.md`.

**Steps.**

1. The Research Synthesist agent processes the new resource
2. Output: source-field synthesis (insight, mechanism, practice, risk, translation, possible book section, possible product, possible content piece, what to avoid copying, open questions)
3. Disposition: catalog only, Coherence Log entry, Rigor Map entry, or discarded with reason
4. Updates flow to the relevant files

**Founder time.** 30 minutes per resource for review.

### Workflow Eight. Customer Communication.

**Trigger.** Newsletter subscriber replies. Customer inquiries about offerings (when offerings exist). Inbound from social.

**Steps.**

1. Inbound is triaged automatically (Gmail filters or platform-specific routing)
2. Standard inquiries (How do I subscribe? Where do I start? What is Dream Coherence?) are handled with templated responses in brand voice
3. Substantive inquiries are surfaced to founder review
4. Founder responds personally where the response matters

The brand maintains warm correspondence. The founder is present in correspondence at depth, not in volume.

---

## The Notion Workspace Structure

Notion holds the project's operational state. The recommended structure:

```
Dream Coherence Workspace
├── 🌀 Dashboard
│   ├── This week
│   ├── Phase status
│   ├── Key metrics
│
├── 📓 Editorial Calendar (Database)
│   ├── Properties: Surface, Format, Status, Date, Theme, Word count
│   ├── Views: This week, Drafts, Approved, Scheduled, Published
│
├── 🌱 Founder Seed (Database)
│   ├── Properties: Date, Format (voice/written), Themes, Status (raw/synthesized)
│   ├── Links to derived pieces
│
├── 📚 Coherence Log (Database)
│   ├── Properties: Date, Entry, Applies to file, Status (active/integrated/dispositioned)
│
├── 📖 Resource Catalog (Database)
│   ├── Properties: Type, Title, Author, Status, Synthesis link, Disposition
│
├── ✦ Aphorism Library (Database)
│   ├── Properties: Aphorism, First used, Times used per surface, Tag
│
├── 🎁 Offerings Pipeline (Database)
│   ├── Properties: Offering, Layer, Status (concept/scoping/building/beta/live), Launch date
│
├── 🎧 Audio Production (Database)
│   ├── Properties: Title, Type, Status (script/recording/edit/published), Surface
│
├── 👥 Practitioners (Database, Phase 2+)
│   ├── Properties: Name, Email, First touch, Offerings purchased, Notes
│
├── 📅 Calendar
│   ├── Phase milestones
│   ├── Launch dates
│   ├── Founder presence events
│
└── 🗂 Working Files
    ├── Book draft folder
    ├── Brand assets folder
    ├── Strategic documents folder
```

---

## The First 30 Days of Infrastructure Build

Specific actions to make the infrastructure operational.

### Days 1-7. Setup.

- Create Notion workspace using the structure above
- Connect Notion MCP to Claude
- Verify Google Drive, Google Calendar, Gmail MCPs are working
- Set up founder seed material capture process (voice note app + transcript automation)
- Create the editorial calendar database
- Load the starting aphorism library (12-20 aphorisms)

### Days 8-14. Pipeline Test.

- Founder records first week of seed material (~45 min)
- Claude session runs the synthesis workflow
- Founder reviews the output
- Refine the synthesis prompt based on what worked and what did not
- Schedule first batch of content to platforms (manually if scheduling tools not yet connected)

### Days 15-21. Newsletter Launch.

- Beehiiv account created and branded
- First newsletter issue drafted by infrastructure
- Founder reviews, edits, approves
- Newsletter ships to founding subscribers (founder's direct network, ~50-200 people)

### Days 22-30. Cadence Begins.

- Full pipeline runs for two consecutive weeks
- Adjustments made based on what is working
- Scheduling tools connected (Buffer or Hypefury)
- First audio piece in production
- Day 30 review: infrastructure operational, cadence holds

---

## Quality Discipline

The infrastructure produces output. The output is held to the brand voice or sovereign voice depending on the surface. Quality controls:

**The Voice Guardian runs on every output.** Before any piece reaches founder review, it passes through the Voice Guardian agent. Output that fails is rebuilt before founder time is spent on it.

**The brand truth test.** Every social post, every newsletter line, every audio script passes the brand truth test before publication.

**The founder veto is absolute.** Any output the founder declines is killed. No automated overrides. No "publish anyway" defaults.

**Periodic recalibration.** Every monthly review includes a sample audit of recent output. If quality has drifted, the prompts and workflows are tightened.

---

## Failure Modes and Discipline

**The infrastructure starts producing average prose.** Discipline: tighten the Voice Guardian's calibration. Reread the voice axioms and the brand guidelines into the synthesis prompts.

**The founder stops generating seed material.** Discipline: the infrastructure can run from the accumulated body of work for ~4 weeks; beyond that, the field starves. The seed material is the engine.

**Cadence breaks for more than two weeks.** Discipline: investigate what broke. Adjust the pipeline. The cadence is the audience's relationship with the work.

**Operational complexity exceeds the founder's capacity to maintain.** Discipline: simplify. The infrastructure exists to serve the founder, not the reverse. If it requires more than 8 hours per week from the founder to operate, simplify until it does not.

---

## The Standard

The infrastructure is invisible when it is working. The audience experiences a single, coherent body of work appearing in disciplined cadence across the brand's surfaces. The founder experiences a small set of clear weekly inputs and outputs that compress what would be unsustainable solo work into something rhythmic and real.

The infrastructure is the field's circulatory system. The work is the body.

◯

# FILE: 28_FOUNDER_PRESENCE_PROTOCOL.md

---

# Dream Coherence. Founder Presence Protocol.

**Version 1.2. May 2026. Working Voice register. Supersedes v1.0. The discipline of when and how the founder appears.**

This file defines the relationship between the faceless brand and the founder's selective presence. The brand is the work. The founder is the steward. The two are not the same. This file holds the discipline that keeps them clearly distinguished.

---

## The Frame

Dream Coherence as a brand is faceless. This is locked in the brand guidelines and is non-negotiable at the brand level. The mark is the brand's face. The voice is the brand's signature. The work is the brand's identity.

The founder is a real person who is named on the book, who exists in the world, and whose lived practice is the deepest validation of what the work transmits. The founder appears at high-leverage moments where presence supports the work, and is otherwise private.

Faceless brand. Named founder. Selective appearance. Stewardship model.

---

## What the Founder Is

The founder is the source of the recognition the work transmits, the lived case study the work points to, the voice that calibrates the work's register, and the steward of the architecture as it grows.

The founder is not the brand. The brand existed before the founder appeared publicly and will continue if the founder ever steps back from public presence. The work is larger than any individual.

The founder is not the personality. There is no founder content stream parallel to the brand content stream. The founder does not have a personal social media presence built off the brand.

The founder is not the marketing engine. The work markets itself. The founder appears to add depth at moments where presence carries weight.

---

## When the Founder Appears

A small set of high-leverage moments. Each is named, scoped, and held to discipline.

### One. On the book.

The founder's name appears on the book as author. This is the primary public identification of the founder with the work. The book is the foundational artifact and the convention of authored books is that the author is named. The brand carries everything else; the book carries the founder's name.

Cover treatment per the brand guidelines: author name in Inter, 11-12pt, letter-spacing +4, all caps, in Moon on the Night ground. Subordinate to the title and the brand label, not centered as the primary identifier.

### Two. Opportunistic interviews.

When podcast and interview opportunities arrive that align with the work, the founder takes them. The architecture does not concentrate these into a launch window or treat them as a critical leverage moment. The work happens regardless.

The discipline of which interviews to take is in the *Decision Standard* section below. The default is to decline. Most opportunities do not meet the standard.

Aligned venues are serious contemplative and intellectual podcasts. Examples in the work's territory: serious long-form conversation venues with editorial seriousness, contemplative-tradition podcasts, intellectual deep-dive shows. Not mass-market shows. Not wellness shows. Not motivational-speaker venues.

When interviews land, they are honored. When they do not, the work continues.

### Three. The deep container.

When the high-end container (Layer Four of the offerings ladder) is running, the founder is the practitioner the participants engage directly. This is one of the few places where the founder's direct presence is the offering.

The container is small. Twenty to fifty practitioners at any time. Application-only. Selected by the founder's reading of who is ready (calibrated against `30_PRACTITIONER_PROFILE.md` and screened per `10_QUALITY_SAFETY_AND_RED_TEAMING.md`). The founder's involvement is real and sustained throughout the container.

### Four. The deepest correspondence.

The founder personally responds to a small set of correspondence each week. Newsletter subscribers who write substantive replies. Practitioners in the high-end container. Selected inbound from people whose work has affinity with the work. This correspondence is private, never reproduced publicly without explicit permission, and is the relational ground the work maintains with its deepest practitioners.

### Five. Strategic conversations.

The founder occasionally appears in strategic settings where presence is required. Negotiation with a publisher. Partnership conversations with serious institutions. Collaborations with adjacent practitioners whose work has affinity. These are private by default.

---

## The Discipline of Personal Story

The founder's life — partnership, fatherhood, dharma, individuation, lived practice — is the lived ground of the work. The recognition is not theoretical; it is lived in a specific life with specific relationships and specific responsibilities.

Personal story enters the work only when the lesson translates and the teaching becomes better as a result. The discipline:

**The default is privacy.** The brand's faceless register does not invite personal disclosure. Newsletter issues, social posts, audio pieces, and essays operate without biographical specifics about the founder unless the specific story carries the teaching.

**When personal story enters, it enters lightly and in service of the lesson.** A passage may reference parenting a small child, or being in a long partnership, or a moment in lived practice, when that reference is the most precise way to transmit a recognition. The reference does not become the subject of the piece. The recognition remains the subject.

**Names of people in the founder's life are protected.** Lalita and Satya are not named in any public-facing content. References, when made, use formulations like "my partner," "my child," "the small person living in my house." This protects their privacy and keeps the writing in the territory the work occupies.

**Specific events are protected by default.** A piece may use a parenting moment to transmit a recognition about identity, but the piece does not become a parenting memoir. A piece may reference a moment of relational difficulty to transmit something about the dream of the self, but the piece does not become a relationship essay.

**The filter is: would this still be in the piece if the founder were anonymous?** If yes, the personal reference is doing real work. If no, the reference is self-indulgent and gets cut.

**Self-realization is the work, not self-expression.** The founder's lived dimension serves the recognition. The recognition does not serve the founder's self-expression. When the filter is unclear, default to leaving the personal reference out.

This discipline runs alongside the faceless brand requirement. The brand stays faceless. The founder, when occasionally referenced through the personal-as-instructive lens, is referenced lightly and only in service of the work.

---

## When the Founder Does Not Appear

A discipline-clarifying list of what the founder does not do.

### Personal social media.

The founder does not have a personal X, Instagram, TikTok, or LinkedIn presence built off the brand. The founder may have private accounts under different names for personal use, kept separate from the brand entirely. There is no "founder content" on the brand surfaces.

### Day-to-day brand presence.

The brand voice is the brand's voice. The founder is not the voice. Newsletter issues, social posts, and other brand outputs are not signed by the founder, do not reference the founder, and do not carry the founder's biographical details — except in the carefully filtered way described in *The Discipline of Personal Story* above.

### Mass-market press.

The founder declines mass-market press appearances. Mainstream morning television. Lifestyle magazines. Daily news circuits. These do not serve the work and dilute the founder's presence.

### Sponsored content of any kind.

The founder does not endorse products, accept sponsored content, or appear in advertising for other brands. The founder's presence is reserved for the work.

### Reactionary content.

The founder does not respond publicly to critics, controversy, or trending discourse. The work speaks. The founder does not.

### Influencer collaborations.

The founder does not appear in collaboration posts, podcast swaps that are primarily promotional, or other influencer-economy patterns.

### Photography and video for the brand.

The brand's imagery is atmospheric, symbolic, faceless per the brand guidelines. The founder is not in brand photography. The founder's face does not appear on the website (except possibly a small author photo if a publisher requires it for the book). The brand visuals never picture the founder.

---

## How the Founder Is Named

Naming conventions:

- On the book: real name, as agreed.
- In bylines on long-form essays the founder personally writes: real name, where the founder's voice is the point. Most essays are published unsigned under "Dream Coherence" since they emerge from the brand voice work.
- In interview attributions: real name with affiliation to Dream Coherence.
- In direct correspondence: signed personally by the founder.
- In any introduction or third-party reference: real name with affiliation.

The founder does not adopt a pseudonym for the work. The faceless brand does not require an invented name for the founder; it requires that the founder not become the primary public surface of the work.

---

## The Author Photo Question

If the book is published traditionally, the publisher will likely request an author photo. Discipline:

- Author photo is optional. The founder may decline if the publisher allows it.
- If required, the photo is taken in a register aligned with the brand. Atmospheric, deeply lit, contemplative. Not headshot-on-white. Not professional-author-photo. Closer to the visual register of the brand guidelines.
- The photo appears only in publisher-controlled contexts (book jacket, publisher's author page, possibly book retailers). It does not appear on the Dream Coherence website. It is not used for the brand's own marketing.

If the book is self-published, no author photo is required and none should be made.

---

## Biographical Notes

When the founder requires a biographical note (for podcast intros, book back-matter, interview platforms), the note is calibrated to the brand voice and stays minimal.

**Short form (for podcast intros, social bios):**
> [Founder name] is the source of Dream Coherence, a contemplative protocol for sleep, lucidity, and reality alignment.

**Medium form (for interview platforms, book back-matter):**
> [Founder name] is the source of Dream Coherence, a contemplative protocol for sleep, lucidity, and reality alignment. The work draws on the convergence of Kashmir Shaivism, Dzogchen, Daoist contemplative tradition, Kabbalah, and contemporary cognitive science, distilled into a method for waking inside the dream of life.

**Long form (for substantive interview placements):**
> The medium form, plus 2-3 sentences about the lived ground of the work. Carefully calibrated to avoid biographical specifics that pull focus from the work. The founder is described by what the founder does, not by where the founder went to school or what the founder did before this.

The biographical notes do not include credentials, certifications, lineage claims, or self-aggrandizing language. The work is the credential.

---

## Decisions Held for Later

Several questions are deferred to specific moments when the answer becomes obvious.

**Whether to use a single chosen full name or initials.** Decided when the book moves to production. Some authors of contemplative work use initials (J.M. Coetzee model) to support a slight distance between personal life and public work. The founder decides at the time.

**Whether the founder ever does long-form video appearances.** Held until specific opportunities arise. If a high-trust long-form conversation venue arises, the founder may appear. Defaults to no unless the venue is at the register of the work.

**Whether the founder names the lineage of their own contemplative practice publicly.** Likely held private. The work draws from multiple streams; the founder's specific teachers, retreats, and influences are personal. May change if a public articulation serves the work at a specific moment.

**Whether the founder appears in person at retreats (if retreats enter the architecture).** Decided when retreats are scoped, with the discipline that founder presence at retreats is the highest-leverage in-person work and should be reserved for the deepest practitioners.

---

## The Decision Standard

When in doubt about whether the founder should appear in a specific context, ask:

- Does this serve the recognition the work transmits?
- Does this serve the field's growth at depth, not just at surface?
- Is the venue at the register of the work?
- Will this presence add weight or dilute presence?
- Will appearing here mean declining a more aligned opportunity later?

If the answers are yes / yes / yes / add weight / no, appear. If any answer is no, decline.

The default is to decline. Selective presence requires saying no more than yes.

---

## The Closing Frame

The founder is the source of the work and the steward of its arc. The founder is not the personality the work circulates around. The brand carries the work. The founder appears when presence matters and is private when presence does not matter. Personal life enters the work only when the teaching becomes better as a result and never as self-expression.

Held with discipline, this protocol protects both the work and the founder's life. The work grows on its own legs. The founder remains free to live the practice that the book transmits, which is the deepest contribution the founder can make.

The face stays the spiral. The voice stays the brand. The founder stays the steward.

◯
