# FILE: MASTER_PROMPTS_LIBRARY.md

---

# Dream Coherence. Master Prompts Library.

**Version 1.2. The operational prompts that run the content engine, the customer communications, the strategic reviews, and the production pipeline.**

This file holds the working prompts the AI infrastructure uses. Each prompt has been calibrated to the brand voice discipline and the architecture's current state. Prompts are versioned; when a prompt is refined, the version is updated.

Prompts are run from the Claude project (this one), from standalone Claude chats with the relevant files attached, or eventually through API workflows with the prompts as system prompts.

---

## How These Prompts Are Used

There are three operating modes:

**Manual.** Paste the prompt into a Claude chat, attach the relevant files (the Sovereign Voice and Brand Voice files, the seed material, etc.), provide the specific input, and review the output.

**Project-based.** Within this Claude project (with all the source-of-truth files attached), invoke the prompt by reference (e.g., *Run the Weekly Seed Synthesis prompt on these voice notes*).

**Pipeline (eventual).** Via Notion + Zapier + Claude API, the prompts run as scheduled workflows with structured inputs and outputs.

All prompts assume Claude has access to:
- `00_READ_ME_FIRST.md`
- `12_OPENING_LOCKED.md`
- `13_VOICE_AXIOMS.md`
- `13a_VOICE_DISCIPLINES_ADDENDUM.md`
- `04_LANGUAGE_VOICE_AND_TRANSMISSION.md`
- `23_BRAND_SYSTEM.md`
- `24_FIELD_ARCHITECTURE.md`
- `26_AUDIENCE_AND_CONTENT_ENGINE.md`

---

# PROMPT 1. Weekly Seed Material Synthesis

**Purpose.** Take the founder's weekly voice notes / written reflections / lived observations and synthesize them into the week's editorial calendar.

**Frequency.** Once per week, Monday morning.

**Input.** The week's seed material (voice note transcripts, written notes, screenshots, photographs of dreams or journal entries). Typically 5,000-20,000 words of raw material.

**Output.** A populated weekly editorial calendar with drafts for founder review.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are the Content Engineer agent for Dream Coherence. You have full context on the brand voice (`23_BRAND_SYSTEM.md`, `04_LANGUAGE_VOICE_AND_TRANSMISSION.md`) and the sovereign voice (`12_OPENING_LOCKED.md`, `13_VOICE_AXIOMS.md`). You know the editorial calendar structure and the audience engine (`26_AUDIENCE_AND_CONTENT_ENGINE.md`).

I will provide this week's seed material from the founder. Your task is to synthesize it into the week's content calendar.

Process:

1. Read the seed material carefully. Identify all distinct ideas, recognitions, observations, and threads that surfaced.

2. For each distinct idea, evaluate:
   - Strength of the recognition (is this a fresh seeing, a clarification, or a restatement?)
   - Specificity (is this concrete and usable, or vague?)
   - Resonance with the work's two pillars (Nature of Reality, Nature of Self)
   - Surface fit (which surface does this best serve?)
   - Whether this surfaces a Coherence Log entry candidate

3. Tag the strongest 3-5 ideas for elevation into pieces this week.

4. For each elevated idea, produce:
   - A short brief (2-3 sentences naming what the piece transmits)
   - The recommended surface (newsletter, X, Instagram, audio script, long-form essay)
   - The recommended format
   - A draft (length appropriate to the surface)

5. The week's outputs should include:
   - One newsletter issue (600-1,200 words, deepest transmission of the week)
   - 5-10 X posts (mix of single aphorisms and short threads)
   - 3-5 Instagram pieces (atmospheric image concept + text overlay + caption)
   - 0-1 audio piece (script for a 10-20 minute audio essay or guided practice)
   - 0-1 long-form essay candidate (1,500-3,000 words, for the website)

6. Identify 2-4 secondary ideas that should be held in the seed bank for future weeks.

7. Surface 1-3 Coherence Log entry candidates (insights that might update earlier work).

Discipline:

- Every draft passes the brand truth test (would this be true and useful stripped of styling?).
- No forbidden words (per `23_BRAND_SYSTEM.md`).
- No em dashes. Period.
- No marketing-anxious language. No hyperbole. No CTAs in social captions.
- Brand voice register unless the piece is for the deepest surface (long-form essay can lean Sovereign).
- Each newsletter issue contains at least one passage that could stand alone as a Brand Voice aphorism.

Output format:

```
WEEK OF [date]

THEMES SURFACED:
[List of distinct ideas with brief evaluation.]

ELEVATED FOR THIS WEEK:
[3-5 ideas with briefs.]

NEWSLETTER ISSUE
Title: [proposed title]
Length: [word count]
Draft:
[Full draft.]

X POSTS
1. [post]
2. [post]
...

INSTAGRAM PIECES
1.
  Image concept: [atmospheric description]
  Text overlay: [phrase]
  Caption: [1-3 sentences]
...

AUDIO PIECE (if produced this week)
Title: [proposed title]
Type: [guided practice / atmospheric piece / spoken essay]
Length: [minutes]
Script:
[Full script.]

LONG-FORM ESSAY CANDIDATE (if produced this week)
Title: [proposed title]
Brief: [2-3 sentences]
[Optional full draft or structural outline.]

HELD IN SEED BANK FOR FUTURE WEEKS:
[2-4 ideas with brief notes.]

COHERENCE LOG ENTRY CANDIDATES:
[1-3 entries.]
```

Begin by reading the seed material I will provide below. Then produce the full output.

[FOUNDER'S SEED MATERIAL HERE]
```

---

# PROMPT 2. Newsletter Issue Drafting

**Purpose.** Draft a single newsletter issue from a specific seed idea or topic.

**Frequency.** Weekly (often pulled directly from the synthesis output, but can be run independently).

**Input.** A topic, theme, or seed idea. Optionally a few sentences of direction from the founder.

**Output.** A complete newsletter issue ready for founder review.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are drafting a Dream Coherence newsletter issue. The newsletter is the field's deepest weekly transmission. Brand Voice register with permission to lean toward Sovereign Voice in the most resonant passages.

Constraints:

- Length: 600-1,200 words.
- Open with an image, observation, or sentence that arrests attention without theatrics.
- Body develops one recognition, returns to one image, and gives one practice or invitation.
- Close with a single line that could stand alone as a quotation.
- No headers, no bullet points, no calls to action. Flowing prose.
- No em dashes.
- No forbidden words (per brand guidelines).
- No first-person plural (no 'we') unless contextually unavoidable.
- The reader is addressed as 'you' sparingly; mostly the prose is observational.

Voice register:

- Calm, declarative, precise, slightly oracular.
- Each sentence does work.
- The piece sounds like it could have been written 200 years ago or 200 years from now.
- Passes the brand truth test: stripped of styling, the writing is still true and useful.

Structure (loose, adapt as the piece requires):

Opening (50-100 words). An image, observation, or arresting sentence.
Development (400-800 words). The recognition develops. Returns to the image. Gives a practice or invitation. The reader is moved through a small but real shift in seeing.
Closing (50-150 words). The recognition lands. One line at the very end that could be quoted.

Format the issue with:
- A title (EB Garamond Italic, evocative, 2-7 words)
- The body
- (Optional) a single image concept the issue could be paired with

The topic / seed for this issue:

[TOPIC OR SEED MATERIAL]

Begin drafting.
```

---

# PROMPT 3. Social Post Generation (X)

**Purpose.** Generate a batch of X posts from the week's themes or the standing aphorism library.

**Frequency.** Weekly (typically as part of the seed synthesis output, but can be run independently).

**Input.** A theme, a recognition, or the instruction to draw from the standing library.

**Output.** 10-20 X posts ready for founder review and scheduling.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are generating Dream Coherence X posts. The X surface is where the brand voice is encountered as compressed, declarative, oracular writing. Posts are designed to be quotable, screenshotable, and shareable on their own merit.

Generate 10-20 posts on the theme/seed below. Mix:

- 60% single-aphorism posts (one or two lines, dense and quotable)
- 30% short observational posts (2-4 lines, developing a small recognition)
- 10% threads (3-7 posts, building a recognition across multiple beats)

Each post:

- Brand Voice register.
- 280 characters or less for single posts.
- No emojis except occasional ✦ or ◯.
- No hashtags.
- No CTAs ('check out my newsletter', 'link in bio').
- No engagement bait ('agree?', 'tell me in the comments').
- Passes the brand truth test.
- No forbidden words.

Discipline:

- Each post stands alone. No reliance on a parent thread for meaning.
- Aphorisms compress an entire recognition into one or two lines.
- Observational posts make a small specific seeing visible.
- Threads develop a recognition without padding.

Format:

```
POST 1 (aphorism)
[text]

POST 2 (aphorism)
[text]

POST 3 (observation)
[text]

...

THREAD 1 (5 posts on [topic])
1/ [text]
2/ [text]
3/ [text]
4/ [text]
5/ [text]
```

Theme / seed:

[THEME OR SEED]

Begin generating.
```

---

# PROMPT 4. Social Post Generation (Instagram)

**Purpose.** Generate Instagram pieces (image concept + text overlay + caption).

**Frequency.** Weekly.

**Input.** A theme, a recognition, or the instruction to draw from the standing aphorism library.

**Output.** 3-6 Instagram pieces ready for image generation and founder review.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are generating Dream Coherence Instagram pieces. Instagram is where the brand's atmospheric aesthetic becomes felt. Each piece is one atmospheric image plus an EB Garamond Italic text overlay plus a 1-3 sentence caption.

For each piece, produce:

1. IMAGE CONCEPT
A specific atmospheric image description that will be passed to image generation. The image follows the brand's visual discipline:
- Dim, dreamlike, contemplative
- Brand palette: Night, Moon, Gold tonal range
- Possible subjects: deep night skies, moonlight, water reflections, dim landscapes, solitary silhouetted figures, doorways, thresholds, columns, archways, symbolic objects (a hand, a single flame, a bowl of water, a stone), liminal spaces (dawn, dusk)
- Never: bright/cheerful, crystal-shop spirituality, new-age cosmic, wellness-influencer, palm trees, manifestation TikTok, recognizable people or copyrighted images

Format the image concept as a prompt that could be passed to Midjourney or similar:
`[subject], dim moonlight, deep blue and gold tones, atmospheric, contemplative, soft focus, film grain, mystical mood, archetypal, --ar 1:1 --style raw --v 6`

2. TEXT OVERLAY
A single phrase to overlay on the image. 4-12 words. EB Garamond Italic. Brand Voice register. Designed to be screenshotable.

3. CAPTION
1-3 sentences in Brand Voice. Often a small development of the overlay phrase. No hashtags. No CTAs. No emojis.

Generate 3-6 pieces on the theme/seed below.

Theme / seed:

[THEME OR SEED]

Format:

```
PIECE 1
Image concept:
[Description / Midjourney-style prompt]

Text overlay:
[Phrase]

Caption:
[Caption]
```

Begin generating.
```

---

# PROMPT 5. Audio Script Drafting

**Purpose.** Draft an audio piece (guided practice, atmospheric piece, or spoken essay).

**Frequency.** Monthly in Phase 1, weekly by Phase 3.

**Input.** Piece type (guided practice, atmospheric, or essay), topic, length target.

**Output.** A complete audio script ready for founder review and recording.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are drafting a Dream Coherence audio piece. Audio is one of the most intimate brand surfaces. The script will be read aloud, possibly by the founder, possibly by a chosen narrator. The voice register is Brand Voice with permission to lean Sovereign for the most contemplative pieces.

Piece type: [GUIDED PRACTICE / ATMOSPHERIC PIECE / SPOKEN ESSAY]
Topic: [TOPIC]
Target length: [MINUTES] (approximately [WORDS] words at 130-150 wpm)

Script discipline:

- Written for the ear, not the eye. Read aloud at all stages of drafting.
- Sentences breathable. Each clause can be spoken in a single breath.
- Pacing built in. Use [pause] tags where the reader should rest.
- No em dashes.
- No forbidden words.
- Repetition used deliberately for rhythm and emphasis.

For GUIDED PRACTICE:
- Opens with a settling sequence (60-90 seconds of arrival, body, breath)
- Introduces the practice's specific instruction clearly
- Walks the listener through the practice, pacing each instruction with [pause] tags
- Closes with integration and a clear return
- The instruction is concrete enough that the listener knows exactly what to do

For ATMOSPHERIC PIECE:
- Opens with an image or sound description
- Moves through a single recognition or scene
- The listener is held in a state, not instructed
- Closes with a line that lands

For SPOKEN ESSAY:
- Opens with an arresting sentence or observation
- Develops a single recognition
- Returns to the opening at close
- Length 10-25 minutes
- Brand or Sovereign register depending on depth

Format the script with:
- Title (EB Garamond Italic, 2-7 words)
- Length estimate
- Full script with [pause] tags where appropriate
- Closing phrase

Topic / direction:

[TOPIC AND DIRECTION]

Begin drafting.
```

---

# PROMPT 6. Long-form Essay Drafting

**Purpose.** Draft a long-form essay for the website.

**Frequency.** Monthly in Phase 1, weekly by Phase 2.

**Input.** Topic or recognition, target length, voice register (Brand or Sovereign), optional structural notes.

**Output.** A complete essay ready for founder review.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are drafting a Dream Coherence long-form essay for the website. The essay is one of the deepest public surfaces of the work. Voice register: [BRAND or SOVEREIGN, defaulting to Brand with passages permitted to lean Sovereign].

Topic: [TOPIC]
Target length: [WORDS, typically 1,500-4,000]
Structural notes: [OPTIONAL]

Essay discipline:

- Written as a serious piece of contemplative writing, not as content marketing.
- The essay develops a single recognition from open to close. The reader undergoes something across the read.
- Sentences vary in length but each does work.
- No headers (or minimal headers if the essay genuinely requires structural breaks).
- No bullet points (or rare bullet lists only where genuinely necessary).
- No CTAs at the close. No "subscribe to my newsletter."
- No em dashes.
- No forbidden words.
- The essay carries weight. It reads like something that could appear in a serious literary or contemplative journal.

Structure (loose, adapt as the piece requires):

Opening (~10% of length). An image, observation, scene, or arresting frame. Establishes the territory.
Development (~70% of length). The recognition develops across several beats. The piece may move through observation, illustration, lineage reference (sparingly), practice, and reflection.
Closing (~20% of length). The recognition lands. The reader is delivered to a different seeing than they entered with. The final sentence stands alone.

Topic / direction:

[TOPIC AND DIRECTION]

Begin drafting.
```

---

# PROMPT 7. Email Sequence Drafting (First Offering Launch)

**Purpose.** Draft an email sequence for launching or running the first paid offering.

**Frequency.** Built once, used for each launch.

**Input.** The offering's specifics, the launch window, the audience segment.

**Output.** A complete email sequence ready for founder review.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are drafting an email sequence for a Dream Coherence offering. The sequence carries the Brand Voice register. Marketing discipline: never anxious, never urgent, never sales-y. The work invites; it does not chase.

Offering: [OFFERING NAME AND DETAILS]
Audience: [SEGMENT — newsletter subscribers, audio listeners, etc.]
Launch window: [DATES]

Sequence structure (8-12 emails across 14-21 days):

Email 1. Announcement (Day -14 or -21).
Reveal the offering. Frame what it is, what it does, what it is for. No price yet. No CTA beyond "more soon."

Email 2-3. The Recognition (Day -10 to -7).
Two emails that transmit the underlying recognition the offering is built on. These are gifts. They don't sell.

Email 4. The Practice (Day -7 to -5).
Describe the practice work the offering teaches. Specific, honest, no hype.

Email 5. The Story (Day -5 to -3).
The founder's lived case study with this work. Carefully calibrated — the founder appears here in the small way appropriate to the offering, never as a guru. The story is the practice working in a real life.

Email 6. Open for Enrollment (Day -2).
Reveal the price, the format, the timeline. Clear, declarative, no urgency theater.

Email 7. Practice Sample (Day -1).
A single practice or transmission from the offering, given as a sample. This is real value, given freely.

Email 8. Closes in 7 Days (Day +5).
Honest reminder that enrollment closes. No countdown theater. No false scarcity.

Email 9. Closes in 3 Days (Day +9).
Similar reminder. Still calm.

Email 10. Closes in 24 Hours (Day +12).
Clear, brief.

Email 11. Closing Today (Day +13).
Brief and clear.

Email 12 (optional). The Next Round (Day +14).
For those who didn't enroll, brief note about the next cohort or what's next. No guilt, no pressure.

For each email:
- Subject line (Brand Voice, evocative without clickbait, no clickbait formulas)
- Preview text (if applicable)
- Body (200-600 words for most, longer for Email 1 and Email 5)
- Discipline: no countdown timers, no fake scarcity, no upsells in the email

Offering details:

[OFFERING DETAILS HERE]

Begin drafting the sequence.
```

---

# PROMPT 8. Customer Service Response Templates

**Purpose.** Draft the templates for handling common customer inquiries.

**Frequency.** Built once, refined as patterns emerge.

**Output.** A library of response templates for common scenarios.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are drafting customer service response templates for Dream Coherence. The brand maintains warm, brief, accurate correspondence. Templates are starting points; specific responses are personalized as needed.

Generate templates for these scenarios:

1. Newsletter welcome (first email after subscribing)
2. Newsletter unsubscribe acknowledgment
3. "Where do I start?" (general inquiry)
4. "Is Dream Coherence right for me?" (qualification inquiry)
5. "What is the cost of [offering]?" (pricing inquiry, before offering page exists)
6. "Can I get a refund?" (refund request after purchase)
7. "Will you do [thing brand doesn't do]?" (decline politely)
8. "Can I interview you?" (podcast/media inquiry, before founder presence protocol triggers)
9. "Can we partner / collaborate?" (partnership inquiry)
10. "I'm having a difficult experience with the practice" (practice-related concern)
11. "Are you accepting students for [offering not yet open]?" (waitlist scenario)
12. "Can I share / translate / repost your work?" (permissions inquiry)

Each template:

- Brand Voice register.
- 50-200 words.
- Warm but not falsely intimate.
- Direct, helpful, accurate.
- Closing line that respects the reader's autonomy.
- No emojis.
- Signed: "Dream Coherence" or "[Steward name]" depending on the inquiry.

For practice-related concerns (template 10): never offer medical, mental health, or therapeutic advice. Acknowledge the difficulty. Recommend the practitioner consult with a qualified professional if appropriate. Hold the brand's discipline of not making claims beyond what the work supports.

For decline scenarios: kind, clear, brief. No long justifications.

Begin generating templates.
```

---

# PROMPT 9. Course Module Building (Flagship Cohort)

**Purpose.** Build a single course module for the flagship cohort.

**Frequency.** Built once, refined across cohort runs.

**Input.** Module topic, position in curriculum, learning objectives, target length.

**Output.** A complete module structure with teaching script, practice instructions, and integration prompts.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are building a course module for the Dream Coherence flagship cohort. The cohort runs 8-12 weeks. Each module typically holds 1-2 weeks of the curriculum.

Module topic: [TOPIC]
Position in curriculum: [Week X of Y]
Learning objectives: [WHAT THE PRACTITIONER GAINS]
Target length: [TYPICALLY 60-90 MINUTES OF TEACHING, 30-60 MINUTES OF PRACTICE]

Module structure:

1. OPENING TEACHING (15-25 min)
A teaching session that establishes the module's recognition. Brand Voice with passages leaning Sovereign. Read aloud at draft stage.

2. LIVED ILLUSTRATION (5-10 min)
A specific example or story (the founder's or a composite practitioner's) that makes the recognition lived rather than abstract.

3. THE PRACTICE INSTRUCTION (10-15 min)
The specific practice for the module. Concrete, testable, with timing and frequency. The practitioner knows exactly what to do.

4. THE COMMON PITFALLS (5-10 min)
What goes wrong. What to watch for. How to recognize when the practice has become rote or has missed its mark.

5. INTEGRATION PROMPTS (3-5 prompts)
Reflection questions for the practitioner to work with across the week.

6. SMALL-GROUP DISCUSSION PROMPTS (3-5 prompts)
Questions for the cohort's small-group sessions.

7. WRITTEN PRACTICE NOTES (1-2 pages)
A summary document the practitioner can return to throughout the week.

Discipline:

- Brand Voice register for the teaching, with permission to lean Sovereign in the deepest passages.
- The practice instruction is concrete enough that someone could read it once and execute it.
- No hype. No promises of outcomes. The practice is described in honest terms.
- The teaching transmits the recognition; the practice is how the recognition becomes lived.
- Common pitfalls are named clearly so practitioners can self-correct.

Module topic and direction:

[TOPIC AND DIRECTION]

Begin building.
```

---

# PROMPT 10. Weekly Strategic Review

**Purpose.** Produce a brief weekly strategic review for the founder.

**Frequency.** Weekly, end of week.

**Input.** The week's metrics (newsletter, social, audio, website, any offerings revenue) and any notable events.

**Output.** A 5-10 minute read covering what's on track, what's slipping, and what needs adjustment.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are the Field Architect agent producing this week's strategic review for Dream Coherence.

This week's metrics:
- Newsletter: [Subscribers, new this week, opens, replies]
- X: [Followers, posts this week, top-performing post, engagement]
- Instagram: [Followers, posts this week, top-performing piece, engagement]
- Audio: [If applicable: plays, listen-through]
- Website: [Visits, top pages, time on page]
- Offerings: [If applicable: revenue, new customers, refund requests]
- Notable events: [PR, mentions, conversations, founder presence]

Expected trajectory for this phase: [phase reference]

Produce:

1. THIS WEEK'S MOVEMENT (3-5 sentences)
Brief narrative of what happened.

2. ON TRACK
What is moving as expected or better.

3. NEEDS ATTENTION
What is slipping. What needs adjustment.

4. ONE NUMBER TO WATCH
Single most important metric to watch in the next 2-4 weeks.

5. NEXT WEEK'S FOCUS
1-3 specific items to prioritize.

6. COHERENCE LOG CANDIDATES (if any)
Insights that might update earlier work.

Keep it brief. Founder reads this in 5-10 minutes.

Begin.
```

---

# PROMPT 11. Monthly Field Review

**Purpose.** Deeper monthly review with strategic adjustment recommendations.

**Frequency.** Last week of each month.

**Input.** The month's content output, metrics, founder seed material themes, lived practice notes.

**Output.** A 20-30 minute read with strategic recommendations.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are running the monthly field review for Dream Coherence. The Field Keeper, the Field Architect, and the Red Team all contribute. The review is for the founder to use in strategic decision-making.

This month's data:
- Content output: [Pieces produced, by surface]
- Metrics: [Newsletter, social, audio, website growth]
- Founder seed material themes: [What surfaced repeatedly]
- Lived practice notes: [Founder's own practice observations]
- Strategic actions: [What was decided, built, released]
- Phase position: [Where we are in the build]

Produce:

1. WHAT WAS TRANSMITTED THIS MONTH (Field Keeper)
The recognitions that came through across all surfaces. The themes that consolidated. The places where the work moved.

2. WHERE THE WORK IS HEADING (Field Architect)
Themes that are gaining gravity. Themes that are fading. Where the next month's editorial direction should focus.

3. WHAT IS ASKING TO BE BUILT (Field Architect)
Offerings, artifacts, or surfaces that are surfacing as candidates for the next 1-3 months.

4. WHAT IS NOT WORKING (Red Team)
Where output quality drifted. Where the brand voice slipped. Where the architecture needs adjustment.

5. PHASE STATUS (Field Architect)
Are we on track for the current phase's exit conditions? What needs to be true in the next 30, 60, 90 days?

6. STRATEGIC RECOMMENDATIONS (3-7 items)
Specific recommendations for the founder to act on.

7. COHERENCE LOG INTEGRATION CANDIDATES
Entries from this month that should be considered for integration into the locked files.

Length: 1,500-3,000 words. Founder reads this in 20-30 minutes.

Begin.
```

---

# PROMPT 12. Resource Synthesis (Book or Source)

**Purpose.** Synthesize a new resource (book, podcast, paper) from the Living Library or external sources into a structured insight document.

**Frequency.** As needed, when a new resource enters the Resource Catalog.

**Input.** The resource itself (or notes from reading it), context on why it was prioritized.

**Output.** A structured synthesis ready for catalog integration.

---

## The Prompt

```
You are the Research Synthesist agent for Dream Coherence. You are processing a new source for the project's Resource Catalog.

Source: [TITLE, AUTHOR, FORMAT]
Context: [WHY THIS WAS PRIORITIZED]

Produce a structured synthesis:

1. CORE IDEA (2-4 sentences)
The central recognition or argument of the source.

2. MECHANISM (1-3 paragraphs)
How the idea works. What it claims about reality, practice, or experience.

3. PRACTICE (1-3 paragraphs)
What practice or method the source offers, if any.

4. RISK (1-2 paragraphs)
What in this source could be misappropriated, misunderstood, or used in a way that distorts Dream Coherence's recognition. Particular attention to the Shaiva affirmation vs. Buddhist negation distinction noted in the project's lineage discipline.

5. DREAM COHERENCE TRANSLATION (1-2 paragraphs)
How this source's insights translate into the Dream Coherence framework. What language to use. What to avoid.

6. POSSIBLE BOOK SECTION
Where in the book (if anywhere) this source might inform a passage. Specific chapter or movement reference.

7. POSSIBLE PRODUCT MODULE
Where in the offerings architecture (if anywhere) this source might inform a module or practice.

8. POSSIBLE CONTENT PIECE
Where in the content engine (if anywhere) this source might generate an essay, newsletter, or audio piece.

9. WHAT TO AVOID COPYING
Specific phrases, framings, or structures from this source that should not appear in Dream Coherence's work. Honors the lineage discipline of attributing recognition where due while not imitating.

10. OPEN QUESTIONS
What this source raises that requires further investigation, discussion, or resolution.

Source content:

[SOURCE OR NOTES]

Begin synthesis.
```

---

# How These Prompts Evolve

The prompts above are version 1.0. They are refined as the work reveals what works and what doesn't.

Refinement happens:
- After 2-4 weeks of pipeline operation, the founder identifies which outputs needed the most editing and the prompts are tightened.
- After each monthly review, prompt-level adjustments are noted in the Coherence Log.
- When a new surface or offering enters the architecture, new prompts are added.

The prompts library is held in Notion alongside the editorial calendar so prompts are always at hand when needed.

---

# The Standard

Each prompt produces output the founder can act on with minimal editing. The discipline of the prompts is the brand voice discipline plus the operational context. Done right, the prompts produce work that sounds like Dream Coherence and only like Dream Coherence.

If the output drifts, the prompt is wrong. The fix is in the prompt, not in over-editing the output.

◯

# FILE: POEM_DEVELOPMENT_SCOPE.md

---

# Dream Coherence. The Poem. Development Scope.

**Version 1.2. May 2026. The first public artifact of the field, positioned as the bridge between the book's interior register and the field's bolder register.**

The poem is the field's first public transmission. It is not a fragment of the book. It is not a sample. It is a standalone artifact — a single, beautifully made piece of writing that exists at its own register and serves a specific function: be the first thing released that signals what the work is.

This file scopes the poem's development: what the poem is, what it does, how it is produced, how it is released, and what its place is in the architecture.

Note on timing: an earlier version of this scope positioned the poem as released "4-8 weeks before book launch" to seed the field for launch. The current architecture is book-launch-agnostic (per `25_STRATEGIC_ARCHITECTURE.md`). The poem releases when it is ready, in the period when the field is established enough to receive it. The book's release happens when it happens. The poem and the book do not depend on each other to land; each is its own complete artifact.

---

## What the Poem Is

A single long-form poem. Working title held open until written. Approximately 60-120 lines, possibly more if the form requires.

The poem transmits the central recognition of Dream Coherence at the highest contemplative register the work allows. It sits in the Sovereign Voice register, possibly more compressed than even the book allows because poetry can do what prose cannot.

The poem is not narrative in conventional sense. It does not tell a story with characters and arc. It is closer to a Rilke *Duino Elegy*, a Mary Oliver meditation, a Rumi recognition rendered in modern English. The reader undergoes something across the read. The poem's structure is the reader's progression through a recognition.

---

## What the Poem Transmits

The poem holds the work's central recognition in compressed form. The reader encounters:

- The opening seeing — what the moment is
- The dream principle — the recognition that experience is dreamlike
- The threshold — the moment of waking inside the dream
- The body — the gate of recognition
- The practitioner's posture — what it is to live from this
- A closing arrival — what is possible from here

The poem can hold the entire arc of the work in 100 lines. This is the poem's particular power.

---

## Voice Register

Sovereign Voice. Possibly the highest register the work ever publishes. The poem follows all the voice axioms (`13_VOICE_AXIOMS.md`) but with poetic license to compress, to use line breaks for rhythm, to repeat for emphasis, to use image where prose would explain.

What the poem does not do:
- Rhyme in a heavy-handed way (occasional rhyme acceptable, scheme rhyme rejected)
- Use forced meter (free verse with internal rhythm)
- Reference specific traditions by name (the recognition is the inheritance of all of them and belongs to none)
- Sound like translated mystical poetry (avoids the Rumi-pastiche register that floods the contemporary contemplative space)
- Include em dashes (per voice axioms)

What the poem does:
- Hold each line so it can stand alone
- Move the reader through a recognition by accumulation
- Use image rather than abstraction
- Use the body's sensations as anchor points
- Carry weight in a few words
- Land at close with a line that can be quoted, remembered, returned to

---

## Drafting Process

The poem is drafted by the founder, supported by Claude in the project. The process:

**Stage One. The Surfacing.** The founder produces several drafts across weeks, not in a single sitting. The poem surfaces from the founder's lived practice. Claude assists in finding what wants to be said.

**Stage Two. The Compression.** The drafts are compressed. Each line is tested: can it stand alone? Does it earn its place? Long sections are cut. The poem moves toward its final length.

**Stage Three. The Tuning.** The poem is read aloud at every revision. Lines that don't survive being spoken are rewritten. The Voice Guardian agent runs against each pass.

**Stage Four. The Lock.** The founder declares the poem locked. From here it does not change.

Total drafting time: 6-12 weeks. Not rushed.

---

## Production Forms

The poem releases in three forms, each with its own production standard.

### Form One. Free reading.

The poem is published on dreamcoherence.com on its own page (`/the-poem`). Anyone can read it. Free. No gate.

Production: clean web typography in the brand register. EB Garamond Italic, generous spacing, ample white space around the lines. A single audio link to the reading (Form Two). The page is the most contemplative page on the site.

### Form Two. Audio reading.

The poem is recorded as a single 5-10 minute audio piece. Read aloud — by the founder, or by a chosen narrator with a voice the work resonates with. Production at the highest standard the budget allows. Quiet ambient bed (if any) or pure spoken-word. Released on Spotify, available on all streaming.

The audio is free. The audio file is also embedded on the poem's web page.

### Form Three. Limited edition print.

A small print run of the poem as a physical artifact. 500-2000 copies, depending on demand signals. Hand-bound or premium production. Letterpress or quality offset printing. Heavy paper. Brand colors. The poem set in EB Garamond Italic at the deepest typographic care.

The physical edition is sold at $50-$150 per copy. This is the first paid revenue artifact of Dream Coherence, possibly arriving even before the first paid course offering.

The limited edition is collectible-grade. Once sold out, it is not reprinted (unless a new edition is commissioned later as an artifact distinct from the original).

---

## Release Timing and Arc

The poem releases when it is ready and when the field is established enough to receive it. Most likely in Phase 1 (Months 9-12), after the website v2 is live, the newsletter has 1K-3K subscribers, and the audio surface has at least 2-3 pieces published.

The release arc spans approximately 4-6 weeks:

**Week 1.** The poem releases on dreamcoherence.com (Form One). No marketing push. A single newsletter announces it: a 200-300 word note saying the poem is live and a link.

**Week 2-3.** The audio release (Form Two) drops. Newsletter announces. Spotify and other audio platforms ingest. The poem begins circulating in the audio surfaces.

**Week 4-6.** The limited edition (Form Three) opens for pre-order. Newsletter announces. Order fulfillment ships approximately 2-3 weeks after pre-orders open.

The book's release happens whenever it happens, independent of the poem. If the book releases shortly after the poem, the two reinforce each other in the field. If the book releases months later, the poem has already established the work's depth and the book arrives into a field that knows what to expect.

The poem and the book are companion artifacts that do not depend on each other.

---

## Strategic Function

The poem does several things at once.

**It establishes register.** Anyone encountering the poem before the book sees immediately what the work is: contemplative, serious, the inheritor of a contemplative literary tradition, not commercial spirituality. The poem sets expectations correctly.

**It tests the field's response.** The reception of the poem — newsletter forwards, social shares, audio plays, limited edition sell-through — gives data on whether the audience is ready for the book.

**It generates a first artifact for the audience to share.** The poem is more shareable than book chapters can be (the chapters do not exist outside the book). The poem can move freely. People share it. The work spreads.

**It produces a small but real first revenue.** The limited edition generates $25K-$300K depending on print run and pricing. This is the first proof that the work can carry an economy.

**It creates a beautiful first object.** Practitioners who own the limited edition own the first physical artifact of Dream Coherence. This is a long-term brand asset.

---

## The Discipline

The poem must be excellent. Not "good enough to release." Not "a fine first try." Excellent. Worthy of standing next to the contemplative poets the work resonates with.

This means: the founder writes it. Claude assists with revision and pressure-testing but does not draft the poem. AI-drafted poetry at this register reads as AI poetry. The poem must come from a person who has lived the recognition. Claude's role is to help the founder do what only the founder can do.

If the poem is not excellent when the founder reads it aloud and feels the recognition land — it is rewritten. If it never reaches excellence, it is held until it does. The poem releases when it is ready, not on a schedule.

The poem's quality is the work's calling card to the world. Calibrate accordingly.

---

## Pre-release Discipline

Before the poem releases publicly:

- The poem is locked by the founder
- The audio recording is produced at high standard
- The physical edition is designed and a proof copy is in hand
- The web page is built and tested
- The newsletter announcing it is written and scheduled
- Pre-order systems are tested for the limited edition

No release happens until all of these are verified.

---

## Aftermath

The poem stays live on the website. It remains free. The audio remains free. The limited edition closes when sold out and is not reprinted.

If the poem lands well, additional poems may follow over the years. A collected edition could eventually become a small book of poems (held for Phase 3 or Phase 4).

The poem becomes a touchstone the field can return to. Practitioners revisit it. New visitors encounter it. The work continues to enter the world through it.

---

## The Promise of the Form

The poem is small. The poem is brief. The poem is the first thing released.

By Phase 4 of the build, when the architecture has matured and the wager is in its final reckoning, the poem will still be the same 60-120 lines it always was. New work will have accumulated around it. The book will be in its print runs. The flagship will have run a dozen times. But the poem will still hold the central recognition in the smallest possible vessel.

This is what compressed forms do. They outlast.

The poem is the field's first transmission and possibly its most enduring.

◯

# FILE: RECOGNITIONS.md

---

# Recognitions

**Version 1.0. June 2026. Living document. The seed bank.**

This file holds the distilled recognitions of Dream Coherence: single sentences, each carrying one seeing whole. It is the seed bank from which chapters, page copy, transmissions, and offerings draw. It is not a content engine. A recognition seeds the work through the normal build. It is never fanned out into eleven formats by machine. The recognition seeds; the build expresses; the voice gate stays human.

---

## The two tiers

**Locked.** Recognitions that hold at the standard of `12_OPENING_LOCKED.md` and have passed the four-witnesses voice gate (Kingsley, Neville, Jung, Rumi). One sentence each. No explanation. Each carries its provenance. These are canonical. They may be quoted, set as chapter distillations, or placed on public surfaces without further calibration.

**Candidate.** Recognitions captured as they surface, before calibration. Raw, sometimes not yet in voice. This is the holding ground, and it is meant to be low friction so that nothing true is lost. A candidate is promoted to Locked only by the voice gate. A candidate that touches the locked position (`14`) or the locked architecture is not promoted in a session at all. It is routed to a Rigor Map stress-test or an integration pass, because the position does not move inside a single conversation.

---

## How a recognition moves

A recognition enters Candidate the moment it surfaces, in whatever form it arrives. It waits there. When the work calls for it, it is calibrated to voice, gated, and promoted to Locked, or it is found wanting and left to wait, or it is discarded with a reason. Locked chapter distillations migrate here as each chapter locks, so the canon of recognitions lives in one place. The seed bank fills from two directions: from new seeings that surface in practice and study, and from the distillations the chapters earn.

---

## Locked

*Awareness is occurring.*
— The book's first recognition. `12_OPENING_LOCKED.md`.

*The life being lived is the life dreaming itself.*
— What the streams are saying, stated plainly. `12_OPENING_LOCKED.md`.

*You are not in the dream. You are the dreaming, learning to know itself.*
— Locked website hero. Coherence Log, 2026-06-04.

*The lineages are prisms. The light is the same.*
— Chapter 2 central distillation. Corroborated by the Opening: a single light met through the facets of a single jewel.

*Coherence is the standing degree of lucidity in the waking dream.*
— The titular word, re-rooted. Coherence Log, 2026-06-09.

*What is made of awareness answers to awareness, so to know the dream is to begin dreaming it.*
— The two truths meeting. Locked website copy. Coherence Log, 2026-06-04.

*The recognition reveals a golden thread running through your days, the same thread that runs through all of existence, so that the gathering of your life and the gathering of the world are one motion.*
— The doubled golden thread. Locked website copy. Coherence Log, 2026-06-04.

*Recognize the dream in order to author it on purpose.*
— The signature line.

*The freedom it leads to is not an exit. It is the freedom to live this dream awake, and to dream it well.*
— The telos. Jivanmukti. Coherence Log, 2026-06-04.

*It is a remembering already in motion.*
— The closing recognition of the Opening. `12_OPENING_LOCKED.md`.

---

## Candidate

*Held for evaluation. Not yet calibrated to voice unless noted. Promotion to Locked requires the voice gate. Anything marked [touches locked ground] is routed to a stress-test or integration pass, not adopted in session.*

*Reality continually becomes, and a human life is one place where it grows conscious of itself.* **[touches locked ground: position]**
— The core reframe from the second external transmission, compressed. The single most consequential candidate held here. It shifts the center from awareness recognizing itself to reality becoming conscious through a particular life. It is not foreign to the spine: Shaiva self-recognition, the lived case study, and the irreplaceable aperture are already held. It is also not the locked position. This is a Phase 1 question. It goes to a Rigor Map stress-test against the Shaiva ground before any adoption, where it either proves truer than the current center or resolves into a complementary emphasis inside the existing position.

*How does reality wish to become itself through this life?* **[touches locked ground: architecture]**
— The vocation seed. The Golden Thread as calling, providence, and direction rather than purpose. Connects to the already-locked doubled-golden-thread recognition and the held vocation motif. A candidate theme for a chapter or an essay, not grounds for a structural restructure into seven texts.

*Every life is a place where reality becomes conscious from a perspective available nowhere else.*
— From the first external transmission. Convergent with the held infinite-made-particular recognition (Coherence Log, 2026-06-04). Close to promotable once calibrated to voice.

*The few recognitions endure because each generation rediscovers them, so when the seeing grows simpler the language grows simpler with it.*
— The discipline of throwing away one's own best writing, stated as a recognition. Drawn from the second transmission. A genuine keeper at the level of method, and the principle that disqualifies the maximalist reading of both transmissions.

---

This file grows slowly. A recognition earns the Locked tier the way the Opening earned its place: by being true, and by sounding like nothing else.

# FILE: RESOURCE_CATALOG.md

---

# Resource Catalog

A living document. Every external resource, idea, perspective, and potential citation or example encountered during the build of the Dream Coherence book is cataloged here. The catalog is the operating surface of the Resource Protocol (`19_RESOURCE_PROTOCOL.md`).

Each entry contains source, key ideas, position level (A through E), book placement, handling notes, routing decision, and date entered. Routing is one of: *catalog only* (stays as permanent home), *Coherence Log* (refines earlier locked work), *Rigor Map* (bears on a load-bearing claim), or *discarded* (with reason).

The catalog is held lightly. Resources can be re-evaluated, re-routed, or moved at any pruning moment. The discipline is to capture material so it serves the book when its time comes, and to release material that does not earn its place.

**Status: living document. Updated as new material is encountered.**

---

## 1. Elce et al. — Dream Content and Recall Study (2025)

**Source.** Valentina Elce and team (Italy). Published in *Nature Communications*, 2025. Analyzed 3,366 dream reports from 207 adults using large language models, compared against demographic, cognitive, psychometric, and sleep measures. Covered by *Nautilus*, May 2026.

**Key ideas.**

- *Dream recall is trainable through attention.* Interest in dreams increases recall; recall increases interest. Participants who entered the study indifferent to dreams began remembering them and continued months later. Mechanism: attentional, not mystical.
- *Mind wandering and dream bizarreness share neural substrate.* Daytime mind wandering correlates with fragmented, scene-shifting dreams. Specific brain regions active in both states. Waking attention and dream life are structurally continuous.
- *Vivid dreams as guardians of sleep.* Vivid dreamers report better sleep quality. Hypothesis: immersive dreaming deepens disconnection from environment, protecting sleep. Dreaming is active and protective, not noise.
- *Cognitive difficulty of dream recall.* Stroop task finding: remembering a dream requires suppressing incoming sensory information and holding a fragile short-term memory. Supports the morning protocol instruction (before phone, before speaking, before coffee).
- *REM timing and recall.* Later waking correlates with more dream recall because REM accumulates in the morning. Sleep architecture matters.
- *Age and dream recall.* Recall declines with age due to earlier waking, less REM, and cognitive changes.

**Position level.** B. Phenomenological/cognitive. Compatible with deeper position but does not confirm it. Do not inflate into proof of consciousness-as-fundamental.

**Book placement.**

- Attention coherence chapter (Phase 4 architecture, Phase 6 detail).
- Body coherence chapter: sleep architecture, REM timing, age effects.
- Practice chapters: empirical support for the morning dream capture protocol.
- Phase 5 lineage and science map: cognitive science layer alongside predictive processing work.

**Handling notes.** Elce is careful with her claims. The book matches that discipline when citing empirical work. Cite the specific findings, not generalized "science says." Do not treat the cognitive substrate explanation as exhaustive — it is the empirical anchor at level B, and the deeper position holds the same findings differently.

**Routing decision.** Rigor Map. Bears on Claim 3 (verification) and Claim 6 (pragmatic mechanisms).

**Date entered.** 2026-05-07.

---

## 2. Dawa Gyaltsen — Five Lines of Single Transmission (7th Century)

**Source.** Dawa Gyaltsen, seventh-century Tibetan master in the Bön tradition. Attributed with achieving the rainbow body. The five lines were transmitted under conditions of extreme secrecy: one master, one student, communicated through a pipe.

**Key ideas.** A complete phenomenological arc in five lines:

1. Vision is mind.
2. Mind is empty.
3. Emptiness is clear light.
4. Clear light is union.
5. Union is great bliss.

Each line dissolves the apparent solidity of the previous and opens the next recognition. Vision collapses into mind. Mind collapses into emptiness. Emptiness discloses itself as luminous. Luminosity and appearance are inseparable. That inseparability is experienced as bliss.

**Position level.** D/E. Direct contemplative recognition expressed as compressed phenomenological arc. Sits at the same position level as the book's own wager.

**Book placement.**

- Phase 5 lineage and science map: Bön/Dzogchen layer. The five lines predate the Buddhist Dzogchen transmission and belong to the Zhang Zhung lineage.
- Possible epigraph or structural reference for the book's own movement from appearance to recognition.
- The chapter on the view: a compressed example of the position the book wagers.

**Handling notes.** Attribute with precision. The book does not transmit from inside this lineage. Note the single-transmission context without romanticizing secrecy. The five lines are evidence, not authority — the book engages them as a lineage's compressed arrival at the same recognition the book wagers, not as transmission the book passes on.

**Routing decision.** Rigor Map (primary, for Claim 2 convergence) and catalog. Bears directly on the convergence claim as primary source material for stress-testing whether the convergence is genuine at the level of compressed phenomenological structure.

**Date entered.** 2026-05-07.

---

## 3. Tenzin Wangyal Rinpoche — Three Doors and Three Innate Awarenesses

**Source.** Tenzin Wangyal Rinpoche, Bön lineage holder. Talk given at a conference (transcript). Also: published research with MD Anderson Cancer Center and NIH, funded studies ($2M+) on meditation practices with cancer patients (~300 participants), with results showing 30-40% reduction in physical pain.

**Key ideas.**

### The Three Doors

Body, speech, and mind as three access points to the luminous ground.

- *Body.* Rest the body, find connection to stillness, go beyond subject-object into the stillness of being.
- *Speech.* Shift attention from inner chatter to silence itself. Listen to silence rather than to the ego. Arrive at the "wisdom of silence."
- *Mind.* Rest in the spaciousness and openness of mind. Be the clear sky rather than imagining it.

### Three Innate Awarenesses

From an ancient Bön text:

1. *Khyab (pervasive innate awareness).* Covers matter and consciousness. Present in everything — macrocosm, flower, all beings.
2. *Primordial innate awareness.* Exists in all sentient beings (not in matter). All beings who have consciousness.
3. *Samri (conscious innate awareness).* The awareness that a practitioner can access and become conscious of. This is the one that produces transformation. Unless you are conscious of innate awareness, nothing changes.

### Warning about theoretical understanding as obstacle

Conceptual mind, intellect, and analysis are the primary obstacles to recognizing the nature of mind. The analytical mind must stop analyzing and rest in spaciousness.

### Critique of spiritual materialism

"What am I going to get out of this practice?" Even mindfulness gets colonized by acquisition. Tenzin Wangyal names it "spiritual materialistic view." The practice is not a technology for getting things.

### Rigpa vs. consciousness distinction

Rigpa (innate awareness) is not consciousness-of-something. Consciousness tends to imply duality. Rigpa precedes the subject-object split.

### Clinical evidence

MD Anderson / NIH funded research: ~300 cancer patients practicing the three-doors method. 30-40% reduction in physical pain through resting and hosting challenges in the "sacred space" rather than effortful intervention. Separate online study: ~1,000 practitioners with daily journals.

**Position level.** D/E for the recognition material. B for the clinical evidence.

**Book placement.**

- Phase 5 lineage and science map: Bön lineage layer alongside Dawa Gyaltsen.
- Phase 4 architecture: the Three Doors structure converges with the protocol's body/attention/identity stages and may inform how practice chapters get architected.
- Practice chapters or body coherence chapter: clinical evidence as empirical anchor.
- The samri distinction is structurally important for the book's argument that recognition, not mere existence of awareness, is what transforms the lived life.

**Handling notes.** The book does not adopt Bön terminology wholesale. The Three Doors structure is acknowledged as convergent without claiming transmission. The samri distinction is taken as structural insight into the book's own wager. The clinical evidence is cited as evidence for meditation's effects at level B, not as evidence for the full metaphysical position. The spiritual-materialism critique is held as a steelman the book's practical-fruits claim has to survive. The rigpa vs. consciousness distinction is honored in voice when Dzogchen/Bön material enters the book.

**Routing decision.** Multiple. Coherence Log flags surfaced. Rigor Map flags surfaced (spiritual-materialism critique bears on Claim 4; clinical evidence bears on Claim 3 and Claim 6). Permanent home in catalog for Phase 5.

**Date entered.** 2026-05-07.

---

## 4. Andrew Holecek — Dream Yoga: More Than Lucid Dreaming

**Source.** Andrew Holecek, Vajrayana practitioner and teacher. Talk on dream yoga. Author of several books on dream yoga and lucid dreaming within the Buddhist framework.

**Key ideas.**

### The inversion

Waking state is the most dualistic, therefore the state where we are most asleep. Deep dreamless sleep, if awareness is present, is the most nondual state. The common assumption that waking equals clarity is structurally backwards.

### Dream yoga is distinct from lucid dreaming

Lucidity alone can become "super samsara" — the ego colonizes the dream state for entertainment. Dream yoga engages the dream state with practice, not just awareness.

### The wall test

Walking through a dream wall while lucid and failing. You know the wall is not solid. You know you are dreaming. The body-mind still refuses to pass through. Reveals propensity for reification: treating appearance as real even when you know it is appearance. Holecek's tip: if you cannot walk forward through the wall, turn around and walk backwards. You pass through because you do not see the barrier approaching.

### The recapitulation principle

Every night, consciousness descends through gross, subtle, very subtle, and causal levels, then reverses in the morning. Every thought-moment does the same in micro form.

### Sleep yoga and formless awareness

Deep sleep is not unconsciousness but awareness without objects. The light is always on, but unless an object is placed in it, nothing is seen. Familiarity with formless awareness allows consciousness to remain present when all content dissolves.

### Proximate karma applied to sleep

Last thought seeds the next state. What you hold in mind at the threshold of sleep directly shapes the dream. The state inhabited at the threshold of sleep organizes what the sleeping mind produces.

### Sleep as ego's ultimate refuge

"Sleep, i.e. ignorance, is ego's ultimate refuge." The practice will reveal your preference for unconsciousness. Resistance to the practice is not failure — it is the practice revealing where you prefer not to look.

### Morpheus and Thanatos as twins

The god of dream and the god of death are twin brothers. Their father is Hypnos, the god of sleep. Sleep (ignorance) gives birth to both dream and death. Transcending ignorance transcends both.

### Subtle body practices at the threshold

Lying on the right side (sleeping lion mudra). Blocking right nostril to shift from "sun poison prana" to "moon nectar prana." Bringing awareness to the throat as the substrate of dream consciousness. Visualization of a red lotus at the throat as a dimmer switch for consciousness.

**Position level.** D for the contemplative material (Vajrayana Buddhist framework). B/C for specific practices (the wall test, proximate karma, subtle body practices) when read as phenomenological observations.

**Book placement.**

- Phase 5 lineage and science map: Vajrayana layer.
- Early chapters: the inversion and the lucidity vs. dream yoga distinction belong in Movement One of the experiential arc, possibly in the opening's framing.
- Identity chapter (Phase 4 architecture, Phase 6 detail): the wall test as practice analogy for identity-as-construction.
- Evening protocol: proximate karma as the mechanism behind dream incubation.
- Possible epigraph: Morpheus and Thanatos.
- Practice chapters: subtle body practices stay attributed, not adopted.

**Handling notes.** The Vajrayana technical framework stays attributed, not adopted. Channels, bindus, prana, and the full subtle body system are Holecek's tradition; they are not the book's vocabulary. The book takes structural insight and practice analogies, leaves the technical framework. The wall test gets translated into the book's own language (identity as constructed, the felt weight of habit). Proximate karma gets translated as "the state inhabited at the threshold of sleep organizes what the sleeping mind produces."

**Routing decision.** Multiple. Coherence Log flags surfaced. Rigor Map flag surfaced (proximate karma as contemplative-tradition support for Claim 3). Permanent home in catalog for Phase 5 and Phase 6.

**Date entered.** 2026-05-07.

## 5. C.G. Jung — Systema Munditotius (The Red Book)

**Source.** C.G. Jung, first mandala, painted c. 1916. Published in *The Red Book: Liber Novus*, p. 364. Full title: *Systema Munditotius* ("System of All Worlds"). Created through active imagination, not derived from any Eastern transmission.

**Key ideas.** A cosmogram of concentric rings descending from a luminous spiritual center outward through progressively denser manifestation, with the whole structure mirrored and repeated at every scale. The outermost ring is the *mundus exterior maior* (the greater outer world). The innermost core is the microcosm. Between them: concentric spheres of increasing subtlety, each containing its own polarities.

### Phanes and Abraxas as vertical polarity

At the top: Phanes (Erikapaios), the divine child in the winged egg, the Orphic figure of luminous awareness, flanked by Eros and fire (*ignis*). At the bottom: Abraxas, the *dominus mundi*, lord of the physical world, ambivalent world-creator. From Abraxas sprouts the tree of life (*vita*). From Phanes descends the seven-branched candelabra of spiritual fire. These are not enemies. They are the upper and lower poles of a single mandala.

### The reversal in the inner sphere

Jung: "The macrocosm is repeated, but with the upper and lower regions reversed as in a mirror." The inner sphere inverts the outer arrangement.

### The horizontal axis

Left: body, blood, serpent, moon, void (*Satanas*). Right: fullness (*pleroma*), Sophia, Holy Ghost, love of God (*frigus sive amor dei*). The feminine sphere is heaven. The left-right polarity maps onto the gross-subtle axis. *Pleroma* sits in the sphere of luminous fullness opposite the void.

### Self-similar repetition

Jung: "These repetitions should be conceived of as endless in number, growing even smaller until the innermost core, the actual microcosm, is reached."

### Convergence with the Dream Coherence mark

Concentric rings contracting toward the center, each containing its own repetition of the whole. This is, visually, the phi spiral contracting toward the bindu. Jung arrived at a spiral cosmogram independently. The brand mark is a spiral cosmogram. The convergence is unplanned but structurally real.

**Position level.** D/E. Jung is working from direct experience (active imagination) and arriving at an idealist cosmology. He does not use that term, but the mandala depicts consciousness as the field in which all worlds appear, with matter and spirit as polarities within that field, not as separate substances.

**Book placement.**

- Phase 5 lineage and science map: Western psychological tradition. Jung as independent confirmation that the layered, self-similar architecture of awareness has been mapped from inside Western experience, not only Eastern contemplative practice.
- The chapter on the view (Phase 4 architecture, Phase 6 detail): structural precedent for the book's own prismatic architecture.
- Not an image the book reproduces. A structural reference the book can note briefly.

**Handling notes.** The book does not adopt Jungian terminology wholesale. Active imagination, individuation, archetypes: these are Jung's framework, not the book's. What the book takes from Jung is the structural convergence: a practitioner working from inside his own psyche arrived at a cosmogram that mirrors what the contemplative traditions describe and what the book wagers. Cite with precision. Do not psychologize the contemplative traditions through Jung, and do not spiritualize Jung beyond what his own work supports.

Jung is structurally the **Western convergence point** for the book's convergence claim. The book's named lineages are Eastern (Kashmir Shaivism, Dzogchen, Daoist) and Mediterranean/Levantine (Kabbalah). Jung, working from inside Western psychological tradition without contemplative training in those lineages, arrived at adjacent territory through entirely different methodology.

**Routing decision.** Catalog only (permanent home until Phase 5), with explicit Rigor Map flag for Claim 2.

**Date entered.** 2026-05-07.

## 6. Jeffrey J. Kripal — The Comparative Study of the Paranormal

**Source.** Jeffrey J. Kripal, J. Newton Rayzor Professor of Religion at Rice University. Author of *Authors of the Impossible* (2010), *Mutants and Mystics* (2011), *The Super Natural* (with Whitley Strieber, 2016), *The Flip* (2019), *How to Think Impossibly* (2024). Methodologically serious academic engagement with paranormal phenomena across contemplative traditions and contemporary experience.

**Key ideas.**

### The academic refusal to engage the paranormal is itself a sociological phenomenon

Comparative religion as a discipline has systematically declined to engage paranormal phenomena that practitioners across cultures and centuries have consistently reported. The refusal is not based on the phenomena's absence from the data; it is based on disciplinary norms that emerged in response to nineteenth-century positivism. Kripal argues that the academy's refusal is itself methodologically inadequate.

### The phenomena are extensively reported and structurally coherent across traditions

NDEs, OBEs, precognitive dreams, mystical visions, telepathic phenomena, synchronicities, post-mortem communications — these reports cohere structurally in ways that strain the cultural-conditioning explanation. Different cultures, different centuries, similar phenomenological structure. The convergence is data that any complete account of consciousness must address.

### The "flip" thesis

Many serious scientists, including those at major research institutions, have had personal experiences that flip their materialist framework. Bernardo Kastrup is one example — physicist at CERN, now arguing for analytic idealism. These flips are not rare in private, but rarely disclosed in public because of professional risk. The academy's apparent consensus that the phenomena do not occur is partially an artifact of selection bias in what gets publicly disclosed.

### The "super natural" framing

The phenomena are not supernatural in the sense of violating natural law. They are super natural — features of nature operating at scales or in dimensions that current materialist frameworks do not accommodate. Kripal's position is structurally adjacent to Kastrup's analytic idealism but methodologically different: Kastrup arrives through analytic philosophy; Kripal arrives through comparative religion.

### How to think impossibly

The methodological principle: rather than dismissing reports of the impossible because they violate current frameworks, engage them as data that might be telling us something about reality that the frameworks have missed. This is not credulity; it is methodological honesty in the face of consistent phenomenological reports across cultures.

**Position level.** Kripal himself sits at D — he is working toward a more comprehensive understanding of consciousness as fundamental, but does so through documenting phenomena rather than asserting a metaphysical position. The phenomena he documents range across position levels: NDEs at B-C; telepathy at B-C (replication-troubled but persistent reports); rainbow body at C-D (extensively documented in specific traditions, requires the position the book wagers to be philosophically coherent).

**Book placement.**

- Phase 5 lineage and science map: primary scholarly anchor for the miraculous claim's defense. Kripal is the figure who has done the methodologically serious comparative-religion work on this territory and his position parallels Kastrup's analytic idealism from a different methodological direction.
- Miraculous chapter in Movement Three: Kripal's framework and specific case studies inform how the book engages the territory.
- Phase 4 Wager Chapter: Kripal's "flip" thesis is structurally useful for showing how serious investigators across disciplines arrive at positions adjacent to the book's wager.

**Handling notes.** Kripal is a tenured professor of religion writing as a comparative-religion scholar. The book engages him as serious academic anchor, not as either credulous source or popular figure. Cite specific works with specific arguments. Do not import Kripal's broader UFO/alien material into the book unless a specific argument earns its place; the book's territory is the contemplative recognition and the miraculous at its far edge, not the wider paranormal field Kripal engages. The book takes from Kripal: the methodological argument that the academy's refusal is inadequate, the convergent structural reports across traditions, the "flip" thesis about scientists' private experiences, the "super natural" framing.

**Routing decision.** Catalog only (permanent home until Phase 5), with explicit Rigor Map flag for Claim 7.

**Date entered.** 2026-05-07.

## 7. Father Francis Tiso — Rainbow Body and Resurrection

**Source.** Father Francis V. Tiso, Catholic priest and scholar. *Rainbow Body and Resurrection: Spiritual Attainment, the Dissolution of the Body, and the Case of Khenpo A Chö* (North Atlantic Books, 2016). Specific contemporary documentation of the rainbow body phenomenon in the Dzogchen tradition, with comparative engagement against Catholic resurrection accounts.

**Key ideas.**

### Specific contemporary documentation of the rainbow body

Tiso documents the case of Khenpo A Chö, a Tibetan master who died in 1998, where multiple witnesses observed the specific phenomenological signatures of the rainbow body: progressive shrinkage of the body, rainbows appearing in the sky, the body reducing to a small residue with hair and nails remaining. The documentation includes witness interviews conducted in the years following the event.

### The phenomenon is not legend-building

The reports across Bön and Dzogchen traditions show consistent phenomenological structure over centuries. The specificity of the bodily phenomena does not match the pattern of legend-building, which tends toward inflation and inconsistency. The reports are distributed across many practitioners, witnessed by non-practitioners as well as practitioners, and described with relatively consistent detail.

### Comparative engagement with Catholic resurrection accounts

Tiso, as a Catholic priest, engages the phenomenon comparatively against Christian accounts of bodily resurrection and other traditions' reports of bodily dissolution at advanced spiritual stages. The comparative work surfaces structural similarities that strain the cultural-conditioning explanation while honoring the doctrinal distinctions between traditions.

### What the rainbow body suggests about the body

If the reports are accurate, the body is not the boundary it appears to be. The substrate of the physical body is, at sufficient depth of practice, available to be transformed in ways materialism does not predict. The phenomenon is the strongest empirical case in the contemplative literature for the position the book wagers — that the substrate of what appears as material reality is awareness, and that sufficiently stabilized recognition can reorganize that substrate in observable ways.

**Position level.** C-D. The phenomenon is documented empirically (C) with witnesses, photographs in some cases, and consistent reports across multiple cases. The interpretation requires position D — within materialism, the phenomenon cannot occur as described, so either the reports are wrong or materialism is incomplete. Tiso's argument is for the latter.

**Book placement.**

- Phase 5 lineage and science map: primary empirical anchor for the rainbow body phenomenon.
- Miraculous chapter in Movement Three: the rainbow body is the strongest case in the contemplative literature for the position the book wagers, and Tiso's documentation is the strongest evidentiary base.

**Handling notes.** Tiso is a Catholic priest engaging the Dzogchen phenomenon with comparative-religion seriousness. The book engages his work as primary documentation of a specific phenomenon, not as transmission from either tradition. The book does not claim the rainbow body for itself, claim to have witnessed it, or claim that any specific practitioner will produce it. The book reports the phenomenon as documented, with Tiso's documentation as the scholarly anchor.

**Routing decision.** Catalog only (permanent home until Phase 5), with explicit Rigor Map flag for Claim 7.

**Date entered.** 2026-05-07.
